Commit Message

[JSW-705] specs: renamed fs to gpu

Currently in specs directory we have fs,
inside that common, fs1 and jgs so renamed fs to gpu
for better code structure.

b/gpu/common/bridge_if.h

@@ -0,0 +1,82 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef BRIDGE_IF_H
+#define BRIDGE_IF_H
+
+#include <stdint.h>
+
+/**
+ * enum bridge_port_id - Bridge port IDs
+ * @BRIDGE_PORT_ID_SOC: SOC Ips include MUC, NUC and MGMT ARC
+ * @BRIDGE_PORT_ID_PMA: PMA
+ * @BRIDGE_PORT_ID_SGUNIT_EP: SGunit endpoint (SGunit on compute die 0)
+ */
+enum bridge_port_id {
+	BRIDGE_PORT_ID_SOC = 0x4,
+	BRIDGE_PORT_ID_PMA = 0x5,
+	BRIDGE_PORT_ID_SGUNIT_EP = 0x8,
+	BRIDGE_PORT_ID_MAX = 0xFF
+};
+
+/**
+ * enum bridge_opcode_id - Opcode IDs supported by FW
+ * @BRIDGE_OPCODE_ACK_PHASE: Ack Phase
+ * @BRIDGE_OPCODE_CST_WAKE: CST Wake
+ * @BRIDGE_OPCODE_IP_READY: IP Ready
+ * @BRIDGE_OPCODE_FUSE_PULL: Fuse pull request
+ * @BRIDGE_OPCODE_FUSE_COMPLETIONL: Fuse pull completion
+ * @BRIDGE_OPCODE_MAX: Opcode max
+ */
+enum bridge_opcode_id {
+	BRIDGE_OPCODE_ACK_PHASE = 0xB1,
+	BRIDGE_OPCODE_CST_WAKE = 0xB2,
+	BRIDGE_OPCODE_IP_READY = 0xD0,
+	BRIDGE_OPCODE_FUSE_PULL = 0xF0,
+	BRIDGE_OPCODE_FUSE_COMPLETION = 0xF1,
+	BRIDGE_OPCODE_MAX = 0xFF
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct bridge_simple_msg - Bridge simple message format
+ * @dest_port_id: Destination port where msg will be forwarded
+ *		  check enum bridge_port_id
+ * @source_port_id: Initiator port id
+ *		    check enum bridge_port_id
+ * @opcode: Opcode, check enum bridge_opcode_id
+ * @tag: Tag
+ * @rsvd0: Reserved field0
+ * @eh0: TODO
+ * @exhdrid: TODO
+ * @eh1: TODO
+ * @sai: Initiator SAI value (ranges from 0 to 31)
+ * @resp: Response
+ *        0x00 --> SUCCESS
+ *        0x01 --> UN-SUCCESS
+ *        0x1x --> NA
+ * @rsvd1: Reserved field1
+ */
+struct bridge_simple_msg {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint64_t dest_port_id: 8;
+			uint64_t source_port_id: 8;
+			uint64_t opcode: 8;
+			uint64_t tag : 3;
+			uint64_t rsvd0: 4;
+			uint64_t eh0: 1;
+			uint64_t exhdrid: 7;
+			uint64_t eh1: 1;
+			uint64_t sai: 16;
+			uint64_t resp : 4;
+			uint64_t rsvd1: 4;
+		};
+		uint64_t hdr;
+	};
+};
+
+#endif /* BRIDGE_IF_H */

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef GPU_CPUCP_ASYNC_IF_H
+#define GPU_CPUCP_ASYNC_IF_H
+
+#include <cpucp_common_if.h>
+
+/**
+ * enum cpucp_die_id - CPUCP die ids
+ * @CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_0: COMPUTE_DIE0
+ * @CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_1: COMPUTE_DIE1
+ * @CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_2: COMPUTE_DIE2
+ * @CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_3: COMPUTE_DIE3
+ * @CPUCP_DIE_ID_IO_0: IO_DIE0
+ * @CPUCP_DIE_ID_RSVD: RESERVED
+ * @CPUCP_DIE_ID_IO_1: IO_DIE1
+ *
+ * TODO: Confirm DIE ID numbers with arch, io and compute die
+ * can be split in two enum if both needs 0 based indexing.
+ */
+enum cpucp_die_id {
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_0,
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_1,
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_2,
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_COMPUTE_3,
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_IO_0,
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_RSVD,
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_IO_1,
+	CPUCP_DIE_ID_MAX
+};
+
+/**
+ * enum cpucp_async_event_id - CPUCP asynchronous event ids
+ * @CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_THERMAL_START: Thermal start event
+ * @CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_THERMAL_END: Thermal end event
+ * @CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_POWER_START: Power start event
+ * @CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_POWER_END: Power end event
+ * @CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_HEARTBEAT: d2h async heartbeat
+ */
+enum cpucp_async_event_id {
+	CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_THERMAL_START = 1,
+	CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_THERMAL_END,
+	CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_POWER_START,
+	CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_POWER_END,
+	CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_HEARTBEAT,
+	CPUCP_ASYNC_EVENT_MAX,
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_async_intr_cause - CPUCP async interrupt cause data
+ * @cause_data: Cause data
+ *
+ * For certain hardware event, FW will only provide single cause data
+ * which can be status register of particular engine. Based on
+ * hw_evt_header, Xe Driver will interpret the cause data.
+ */
+struct cpucp_async_intr_cause {
+	uint32_t cause_data;
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_async_hw_evt_header - CPUCP async hw event header
+ * @die_id: Die ID, refer enum cpucp_die_id
+ * @placeholder1: Placeholder1
+ * @placeholder2: Placeholder2
+ *
+ * TODO: Add header details to uniquely indentify event.
+ */
+struct cpucp_async_hw_evt_header {
+	uint8_t die_id;
+	uint8_t placeholder1;
+	uint16_t placeholder2;
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_h2d_async_event_data - CPUCP Device to Host async event data
+ * @hw_evt_hdr: Hardware event header applicable in case of hw event
+ * @data_placeholder: Data placeholder
+ * @cause: Generic interrupt cause data
+ *
+ * Note: This is just a placeholder or example data structure for D2H async event.
+ * The data structure will be updated once finalized. This defines payload[]
+ * added as part of struct cpucp_d2h_async_event
+ */
+struct cpucp_async_event_payload {
+	struct cpucp_async_hw_evt_header hw_evt_hdr;
+	union {
+		uint32_t data_placeholder;
+		struct cpucp_async_intr_cause cause;
+	};
+};
+
+/* struct cpucp_async_global_code - CPUCP async global code
+ * @evt_id: Event ID, refer to cpucp_async_event_id enum
+ * @hw_evt: HW or non-HW event
+ * @rsvd: Reserved for future use
+ *
+ * This is 32 bit global code decoding sent by FW as part of
+ * struct cpucp_d2h_async_event
+ */
+struct cpucp_async_global_code {
+	uint32_t evt_id: 8;
+	uint32_t hw_evt: 1;
+	uint32_t rsvd: 23;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_CPUCP_ASYNC_IF_H */

+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_packet_reset_subtype - CPUCP_PACKET_RESET subtype
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_RESET_SOFT: Soft reset request
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_RESET_HARD: Hard reset request
+ */
+enum cpucp_packet_reset_subtype {
+	CPUCP_PACKET_RESET_SOFT = 1,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_RESET_HARD,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_RESET_MAX
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_packet_event_subtype - CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT subtype
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT_REGISTER: Register events
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT_DEREGISTER: De-register events
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT_UNMASK: Unmask event
+ */
+enum cpucp_packet_event_subtype {
+	CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT_REGISTER = 1,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT_DEREGISTER,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT_UNMASK,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT_MAX
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_packet_pmt_subtype - CPUCP_PACKET_PMT subtype
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_BUILD: PMT discovery build
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_QUERY: PMT discovery query
+ */
+enum cpucp_packet_pmt_subtype {
+	CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_BUILD = 1,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_QUERY,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_MAX
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_packet_type - CPUCP packet type
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_SET: Set request
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_GET: Get request
+ */
+enum cpucp_packet_type {
+	CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_SET = 1,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_GET,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_MAX
+};
+
+/*
+ * CpuCP Primary Queue Packets
+ *
+ * During normal operation, the host's kernel driver needs to send various
+ * messages to device using CpuCP communication protocol, usually either
+ * to SET some value into a H/W periphery or to GET the current value of
+ * some H/W periphery. For example, SET the frequency of engines and GET
+ * the value of the thermal sensor.
+ *
+ * These messages can be initiated either by the User application or by the
+ * host's driver itself.
+ *
+ * The mechanism allows user application or driver's communicating with device
+ * to send multiple packet requests simulteanously. The device will respond to
+ * request(s) through the CQ not neccessarily in sequence the request is been sent.
+ * Upon receiving response for packet, host needs to check unique id from the
+ * response packet header to map response to request.
+ *
+ * Detailed description:
+ *
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_INFO -
+ *       Fetch information from the device as specified in the packet's
+ *       structure.
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_SENSOR -
+ *       Get/Set sensor information from/to the device as specified in the packet's
+ *       structure.
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_TEST -
+ *       Test packet for CPUCP connectivity.
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_RESET -
+ *	Packet to perform reset based on sub-type
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT -
+ *	Packet to perform events related operation
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_NIC -
+ *	Packet to perform NIC related operation(s)
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_HBM -
+ *	Packet to perform HBM related operation(s)
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_SECURITY -
+ *	Packet to perform security related operation(s)
+ * CPUCP_PACKET_PMT -
+ *	Packet to perform platform monitoring technologies related operation(s)
+ */
+enum cpucp_packet_id {
+	CPUCP_PACKET_INFO = 1,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_SENSOR,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_TEST,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_RESET,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_EVENT,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_NIC,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_HBM,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_SECURITY,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_PMT,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_ID_MAX
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_req_pmt_discovery - CPUCP PMT discovery request packet
+ * packet_id: CPUCP_PACKET_PMT
+ * packet_sub_id: CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_BUILD or
+ *		  CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_QUERY
+ * packet_type: CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_BUILD - Don't Care
+ *		CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_QUERY - CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_GET
+ *
+ * @size: Size of struct cpucp_req_pmt_discovery
+ *	  Do not move this field.
+ * @function_type: PF/VF/Native check enum cpucp_function_type
+ * @pad: padding for 4 bytes alignment
+ *
+ */
+struct cpucp_req_pmt_discovery {
+	uint16_t size;
+	uint8_t function_type;
+	uint8_t pad;
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct msi_vector_info - MSI vector info
+ *
+ * @size: Size of struct msi_vector_info
+ *	  Do not move this field.
+ * @comp_type: component type check enum cpucp_component_type
+ * @start_idx: Vector start index
+ *		PF valid values: 0 to 1023
+ *		VF valid values: 1024 to 2047
+ * @end_idx: Vector end index
+ *		PF valid values: 0 to 1023
+ *		VF valid values: 1024 to 2047
+ *
+ * In case only one MSI index assignment for given component type,
+ * start_idx and end_idx will be of same value.
+ *
+ */
+struct msi_vector_info {
+	uint16_t size;
+	uint8_t comp_type;
+	uint8_t pad;
+	uint16_t start_idx;
+	uint16_t end_idx;
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_msi_info_set - CPUCP MSI info set packet data
+ * packet_id: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO
+ * packet_sub_id: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO_MSI
+ * packet_type: CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_SET
+ *
+ * @size: Size of struct cpucp_msi_info_set
+ *	  Do not move this field.
+ * @function_type: PF or VF check enum cpucp_function_type
+ * @vf_num: VF number check enum cpucp_vf_id
+ *	    Valid only in case of function_type: CPUCP_FUNCTION_TYPE_VF
+ * @length: length of vector info array
+ *
+ * Example usage:
+ * function_type: CPUCP_FUNCTION_TYPE_PF
+ * vf_num: 0 (Don't care)
+ * comp_type: CPUCP_COMP_TYPE_NIC
+ * num_of_entries: 3
+ *
+ * vector_info[]
+ * comp_type: CPUCP_COMP_TYPE_NIC
+ * start_idx: 0
+ * end_idx: 15
+ *
+ * comp_type: CPUCP_COMP_TYPE_MEDIA
+ * start_idx: 16
+ * end_idx: 23
+ *
+ * comp_type: CPUCP_COMP_TYPE_NIC
+ * start_idx: 24
+ * end_idx: 31
+ */
+struct cpucp_msi_info_set {
+	uint16_t size;
+	uint8_t function_type;
+	uint8_t vf_num;
+	uint16_t length;
+	uint8_t pad[2];
+	uint32_t vector_info[];
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_request_packet_generic - CPUCP generic packet request
+ *
+ * @size: Size of struct cpucp_request_packet_generic
+ *	  Do not move this field.
+ * @pad: padding for 4 bytes alignment
+ * @pll_index: PLL index, refer to enum cpucp_pll_index
+ * @pll_pad: PLL structure padding for 4 bytes alignment
+ */
+struct cpucp_request_packet_generic {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint16_t size;
+			uint8_t pad[2];
+		};
+	};
+
+	union {
+		 /*
+		  * packet_id: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO
+		  * packet_sub_id: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO_PLL
+		  * packet_type: CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_GET
+		  */
+		struct {
+			uint8_t pll_index;
+			uint8_t pll_pad[3];
+		};
+	};
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_req_packet_data - CPUCP Device to Host request packet data
+ * @data_placeholder: placeholder
+ *
+ * Note: This is just a placeholder or example data structure for H2D request packet.
+ * The data structure will be updated/removed once finalized. This defines payload[]
+ * added as part of struct cpucp_h2d_req_packet
+ */
+struct cpucp_req_packet_data {
+	union {
+		uint32_t data_placeholder;
+	};
+};
+
+/* struct cpucp_req_global_code - CPUCP request global code
+ * @packet_id: cpucp_packet_id enum specifying packet ids supported
+ * @packet_sub_id: packet specific sub-id (if applicable)
+ * @packet_type: refer to enum cpucp_packet_type (if applicable)
+ *
+ * This is 32 bit global code decoding sent by Driver as part of
+ * struct cpucp_h2d_req_packet
+ */
+struct cpucp_req_global_code {
+	uint32_t packet_id: 8;
+	uint32_t packet_sub_id: 8;
+	uint32_t packet_type: 2;
+	uint32_t pad: 14;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_CPUCP_REQ_IF_H */

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef GPU_CPUCP_RESP_IF_H
+#define GPU_CPUCP_RESP_IF_H
+
+#include <cpucp_common_if.h>
+#include <queue_proto_d2h_resp_if.h>
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_packet_rc - CPUCP packet response
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_SUCCESS: Packet process successfully
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_INVALID_PARAMS: Invalid params received
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_INVALID_SZ: Invalid size received
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_FAULT: Fault in processing packet
+ * @CPUCP_PACKET_NOT_SUPPORTED: Packet not supported
+ */
+enum cpucp_packet_rc {
+	CPUCP_PACKET_SUCCESS = 0,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_INVALID_PARAMS,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_INVALID_SZ,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_FAULT,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_NOT_SUPPORTED,
+	CPUCP_PACKET_RC_MAX
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct pmt_dvsec_info - PMT DVSEC telemetry info
+ *
+ * @size: Size of struct pmt_dvsec_info
+ *	  Do not move this field.
+ * @dvsec_length: length of DVSEC in bytes
+ * @entry_size: entry size in bytes
+ * @tbir: BAR id
+ * @pad: padding for 4 bytes alignment
+ * @offset: Offset in BAR
+ * @dvsec_id: dvsec type refer to enum cpucp_dvsec_type
+ * @num_of_entries: number of entries
+ * @pad2: padding for 4 bytes alignment
+ */
+struct pmt_dvsec_info {
+	uint16_t size;
+	uint16_t dvsec_length;
+	uint16_t entry_size;
+	uint8_t tbir;
+	uint8_t pad;
+	uint32_t offset;
+	uint8_t dvsec_id;
+	uint16_t num_of_entries;
+	uint8_t pad2;
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_resp_pmt_discovery - CPUCP PMT Discovery query response packet
+ * packet_id: CPUCP_PACKET_PMT
+ * packet_sub_id: CPUCP_PACKET_PMT_DISCOVERY_QUERY
+ * packet_type: CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_GET
+ *
+ * @size: Size of struct cpucp_pmt_discovery
+ *	  Do not move this field.
+ * @num_dvsec: Number of DVSEC entries
+ * @pad: padding for 4 bytes alignment
+ * @dvsec_info: DVSEC(s) capability structure
+ *		struct pmt_dvsec_info
+ */
+struct cpucp_resp_pmt_discovery {
+	uint16_t size;
+	uint8_t num_dvsec;
+	uint8_t pad;
+	uint32_t dvsec_info[];
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_resp_pll_info_get - CPUCP PLL info get response packet
+ * packet_id: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO
+ * packet_sub_id: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO_PLL
+ * packet_type: CPUCP_PACKET_TYPE_GET
+ *
+ * @size: Size of struct cpucp_resp_pll_info_get
+ *	  Do not move this field.
+ * @pad: padding for 4 bytes alignment
+ * @div0_freq: Divider 0 output frequency in MHz
+ * @div1_freq: Divider 1 output frequency in MHz
+ * @div2_freq: Divider 2 output frequency in MHz
+ * @div3_freq: Divider 3 output frequency in MHz
+ */
+struct cpucp_resp_pll_info_get {
+	uint16_t size;
+	uint8_t pad[2];
+	uint16_t div0_freq;
+	uint16_t div1_freq;
+	uint16_t div2_freq;
+	uint16_t div3_freq;
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_generic_info - CPUCP_PACKET_INFO/CPUCP_PACKET_INFO_GENERIC
+ * @data_placeholder: Placeholder
+ *
+ * Note: This is just a placeholder data structure for generic info. The data
+ * structure will be updated once info required by driver from FW is been finalized
+ * At that point, the placeholder entry will be removed.
+ */
+struct cpucp_generic_info {
+	uint32_t data_placeholder;
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct cpucp_resp_packet - CPUCP Device to Host response packet
+ * @data_placeholder: placeholder
+ * @generic_info: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO/CPUCP_PACKET_INFO_GENERIC
+ * @pll_info: CPUCP_PACKET_INFO/CPUCP_PACKET_INFO_PLL
+ *
+ * Note: This is just a placeholder or example data structure for D2H response packet.
+ * The data structure will be updated/removed once finalized.  This defines payload[]
+ * added as part of struct cpucp_d2h_resp_packet
+ */
+struct cpucp_resp_packet {
+	struct queue_proto_d2h_resp_packet hdr;
+	union {
+		uint32_t data_placeholder;
+		struct cpucp_generic_info generic_info;
+		struct cpucp_resp_pll_info_get pll_info;
+		struct cpucp_resp_pmt_discovery pmt_dvsec;
+	};
+};
+
+/* struct cpucp_resp_global_code - CPUCP response global code
+ * @rc: Response to packet, refer enum cpucp_packet_rc
+ * @rsvd: Reserved for future use
+ *
+ * This is 32 bit global code decoding sent by FW as part of
+ * struct cpucp_d2h_resp_packet
+ */
+struct cpucp_resp_global_code {
+	uint32_t rc: 8;
+	uint32_t rsvd: 24;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_CPUCP_RESP_IF_H */

b/gpu/common/hl_boot_desc.h

@@ -0,0 +1,293 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2018-2023 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef GPU_HL_BOOT_DESC_H
+#define GPU_HL_BOOT_DESC_H
+
+#include <stdint.h>
+
+#ifndef __packed
+#define __packed	__attribute__((__packed__))
+#endif
+
+/* FW boot pointers magic:	  N X T 0 */
+#define HL_FW_BOOT_MAGIC	0x4E585400
+
+/* MAX boot info descriptors */
+#define HL_BOOT_INFO_DESC_MAX_COUNT	20
+
+/* FW image descriptor magic:	  P T R 0 | <hl_fw_img_type> */
+#define HL_FW_IMG_DESC_MAGIC	0x50545200
+
+/* Boot info desc:	  P T R 0 | <hl_boot_info_type> */
+#define HL_BOOT_INFO_DESC_MAGIC	0x50545200
+
+/* HVBH - Habana Volatile Boot Header */
+#define HL_BOOT_INFO_MAGIC	0x48425648
+
+/* Fake fuse magic: Low Voltage Attack - LVA | <fw_boot_pointer_type> */
+#define FAKE_FUSE_BOOT_PTR_MAGIC_NAME	0x4C564100
+
+#define CRC32_NUM_BYTES		4
+#define HL_BOOT_INFO_CSUM_SIZE	32
+#define HL_SHA_384_CSUM_SIZE	48
+#define HL_IMG_VERSION_SIZE	128
+
+enum hl_fw_img_type {
+	HL_FW_IMG_PREBOOT = 1,
+	HL_FW_IMG_PPBOOT = 2,
+	HL_FW_IMG_FAKE_EFUSE = 3,
+	HL_FW_IMG_TPM_SPI = 4,
+	HL_FW_IMG_RECOVERY = 4,
+	HL_FW_IMG_SPI_MAX_BOOT_PTRS,
+};
+
+enum hl_fw_manifest_type {
+	HL_FW_SPI_MANIFEST_0, /* SPI Manifest Image 0 */
+	HL_FW_SPI_MANIFEST_1, /* SPI Manifest Image 1 */
+	HL_FW_SPI_MANIFEST_MAX_NUM,
+};
+
+/*
+ * This index can be used along with boot pointer magic.
+ * Usage 1: N X T 0 | <fw_boot_pointer_type>
+ * Usage 2: L V A 0 | <fw_boot_pointer_type>
+ */
+enum fw_boot_pointer_type {
+	FW_SPI_BOOT_PTR_MAIN,
+	FW_SPI_BOOT_PTR_SUB,
+	FW_SPI_BOOT_PTR_MAX,
+};
+
+enum cpu_block_type {
+	CPU_TYPE_PSOC = 0,
+	CPU_TYPE_MUC = 6,
+	CPU_TYPE_FULLCHIP,
+};
+
+struct hl_fw_boot_magic {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t type;	/* enum hl_fw_img_type */
+			char name[3];
+		} __packed;
+		uint32_t val;
+	};
+};
+
+struct desc_flags {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint32_t mem_init : 1;
+			uint32_t cold_load : 1;
+			uint32_t warm_load: 1;
+			uint32_t sw_rst_load: 1;
+			uint32_t cold_exec: 1;
+			uint32_t warm_exec: 1;
+			uint32_t sw_rst_exec: 1;
+			uint32_t cpu_type: 1;
+		};
+		uint32_t img_flags;
+	};
+};
+
+/*
+ *           struct hl_fw_boot_ptrs                     struct hl_fw_img_desc
+ *            ======================                        =================
+ *          || Boot_ptrs_img_sz     ||              +---->|| Magic           ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || Img_desc_sz          ||              |     || Version         ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || Magic                ||              |     || Offset      [2] ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || Count                ||              |     || Size            ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || Pub key sz           ||              |     || Load H/L        ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || Sig sz               ||              |     || CRC32           ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || Sec ver              ||              |     || SHA384          ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || Key idx              ||              |     || Start H/L       ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || version              ||              |     || DMA DST H/L [2] ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *          || boot_ptrs_sz         ||              |     || FW COMP VER []  ||
+ *          | ---------------------- |              |     | ----------------- |
+ *     +--<<|| Offset2array         ||              |     || CPU CFG BASE    ||
+ *     |      ======================                |     | ----------------- |
+ *     |                                            |     || MEM BASE H/L    ||
+ *     |                                            |     | ----------------- |
+ *     |                                            |     || MEM SIZE        ||
+ *     |                                            |     | ----------------- |
+ *     |                                            |     || FLAGS           ||
+ *     |    Following Array                         |       =================
+ *     |      ======================                |
+ *     +--->|| Offset               ||>>------------+
+ *     |    | ---------------------- |                     =================
+ *     +--->|| Offset               ||>>----------------->|  ...            |
+ *     |    | ---------------------- |                     =================
+ *     :       :                         :                    :
+ *     |    | ---------------------- |                     =================
+ *     +--->|| Offset               ||>>----------------->|  ...            |
+ *            ======================                       =================
+ *
+ */
+
+struct hl_fw_boot_ptrs {
+	/*
+	 * boot pointers image size
+	 * sizeof(struct hl_fw_boot_ptrs) +
+	 * sizeof(offset to descriptor)*count +
+	 * sizeof(hl_fw_img_desc)*count +
+	 * key +
+	 * signature size +
+	 * alignment (0-15 bytes) +
+	 * CRC (4 Bytes)
+	 */
+	uint32_t boot_ptrs_img_sz;
+	/*
+	 * single image descriptor size
+	 * sizeof(struct hl_fw_img_desc)
+	 */
+	uint32_t img_desc_sz;
+	struct hl_fw_boot_magic magic;
+	uint32_t count;
+	uint32_t pub_key_sz;	/* Public key size */
+	uint32_t sig_sz;	/* Signature size */
+	uint8_t sec_ver;	/* Security version of image */
+	uint8_t key_idx;	/* Key index of image */
+	uint8_t version;	/* hl_fw_boot_ptrs version */
+	uint8_t boot_ptrs_sz;	/* hl_fw_boot_ptrs Size */
+	uint32_t offset2array;	/* Offset to array of offsets */
+};
+
+#define HL_FW_BOOT_VERSION	1
+
+struct hl_fw_img_desc {
+	struct hl_fw_boot_magic magic;
+	uint32_t desc_ver;	/* descriptor version */
+	uint32_t spi_offset[2];	/* the offset on the SPI flash */
+	uint32_t size;		/* the size of the image to load */
+	uint32_t load_high;	/* the high [32-63] bits of the load address */
+	uint32_t load_low;	/* the low [0-31] bits of the load address */
+	uint32_t crc32;		/* crc32 hash for integrity check */
+	uint8_t chksum[HL_SHA_384_CSUM_SIZE];	/* sha384 hash */
+	uint32_t start_high;	/* the high [32-63] bits of the start address */
+	uint32_t start_low;	/* the low [0-31] bits of the start address */
+	uint32_t dma_dest_high[2];	/* the high [32-63] bits of spi dma dest address */
+	uint32_t dma_dest_low[2];	/* the low [0-31] bits of spi dma dest address */
+	uint8_t version[HL_IMG_VERSION_SIZE];	/* FW component version */
+	uint32_t cpu_cfg_base;	/* configuration block of the designated CPU */
+	uint32_t mem_base_high; /* high bits [63:32] of the target memory (for memory init) */
+	uint32_t mem_base_low;	/* low bits [31:0] of the target memory (for memory init) */
+	uint32_t mem_size;	/* total target memory size (for memory init) */
+	struct desc_flags flags;	/* flags for controlling automated image loading */
+};
+
+enum hl_boot_info_type {
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_SECURITY_OTP,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_SECURITY_FUSE,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_EN_CAPS,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_KEY_IDX,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PUB_KEY_HASH,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_NVME_STATUS,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_FIT_BOOT_ERROR,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_NIC_EN,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_FW_INFO,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_RMA_HDR_TABLE,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_RMA_HBM_ECC_TABLE,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_RMA_ROW_REP_TABLE,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_FW_UPD_CLOSURE,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_FW_CFG_SKIP,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_BINNING_CONF,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_EEPROM,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_FAKE_SECURITY,	/* Obsolete */
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_LANE_REMAP,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_BINNING_AND_ISO,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PREBOOT_BTL_VERSION,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PREBOOT_EFUSE_VERSION,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PAGE_DISCARD,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_HBM_INIT_DONE,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_ROM_SECURITY_DATA,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_IMG_DESC_MANIFEST_CONTEXT,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_IMG_DESC_MANIFEST_HEADER,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_LINUX_PCR,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_SRAM_REPAIR_DATA,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PREBOOT_VERSIONS,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PID_VERSIONS,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PPBOOT_VERSION,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_FUSE,		/* Obsolete */
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_FAKED_ECC,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_MEM_INFO,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_PALAD_ENGINES,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_EROM_VERSION,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_EVENT_VERSIONS,
+	HL_BOOT_INFO_SECURE_VERSION_NUMBER,
+};
+
+struct hl_boot_info_ptrs {
+	uint32_t magic;		/* magic for validation */
+	uint32_t count;		/* number of offset descriptors */
+	uint16_t desc_ver;	/* descriptor version */
+	uint16_t desc_sz;	/* descriptor size */
+	uint16_t ptrs_sz;	/* struct hl_boot_info_ptrs - self size */
+	uint16_t offsets_arr_off;	/* the offsets array offset from the base of ptrs */
+	/* array of offsets by count size can follow w/o gaps */
+};
+
+struct hl_boot_info_desc {
+	uint32_t magic;		/* magic for validation */
+	uint32_t type;		/* one of enum hl_boot_info_type */
+	uint32_t size;		/* size of data pointed by offset */
+	uint32_t offset;	/* data offset relative to a known base */
+	uint8_t chksum[HL_BOOT_INFO_CSUM_SIZE];		/* sha256 checksum */
+	uint32_t crc32;		/* crc32 checksum */
+};
+
+#define MAX_INFO_DESCS	64
+
+#define BOOT_INFO_SZ	(sizeof(struct hl_boot_info_ptrs) + \
+			sizeof(struct hl_boot_info_desc) * MAX_INFO_DESCS)
+
+/* structure is for use of each project, and is not passed between projects */
+struct boot_info {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			struct hl_boot_info_ptrs ptrs;
+			struct hl_boot_info_desc descs[MAX_INFO_DESCS];
+		};
+		uint32_t data[BOOT_INFO_SZ / sizeof(uint32_t)];
+	};
+};
+
+struct lane_remap_data {
+	uint8_t ln_hbm_mc_pc;
+	uint8_t ln_hbm_dev;
+	uint8_t ln_hbm_type;
+	uint8_t reserved;
+} __packed;
+
+struct lane_remap {
+	uint8_t num_remaps;
+	uint8_t reserved[7];
+	/* array of struct lane_remap_info in count of num_remaps */
+	struct lane_remap_data ln_remap[0];
+} __packed;
+
+struct page_discard {
+	uint8_t num_remaps;
+	uint8_t reserved[7];
+	uint32_t mmu_page_index[0];
+} __packed;
+
+struct hl_fw_desc_info {
+	uint32_t desc_size;
+	uint32_t count;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_HL_BOOT_DESC_H */

+enum cpu_boot_err {
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_INIT_FAIL = 0,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_FIT_CORRUPTED = 1,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_TS_INIT_FAIL = 2,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_SKIPPED = 3,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_NIC_FW_FAIL = 6,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_SECURITY_NOT_RDY = 7,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_SECURITY_FAIL = 8,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_EFUSE_FAIL = 9,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_PRI_IMG_VER_FAIL = 10,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_SEC_IMG_VER_FAIL = 11,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_PLL_FAIL = 12,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_DEVICE_UNUSABLE_FAIL = 13,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_BOOT_FW_CRIT_ERR = 18,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_BINNING_FAIL = 19,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL = 20,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_EEPROM_FAIL = 22,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENG_ARC_MEM_SCRUB_FAIL = 23,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_QUEUE_COMMUNICATION_FAIL = 24,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENABLED = 31,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_SCND_EN = 63,
+	CPU_BOOT_ERR_LAST = 64 /* we have 2 registers of 32 bits */
+};
+
+/*
+ * Mask for fatal failures
+ * This mask contains all possible fatal failures, and a dynamic code
+ * will clear the non-relevant ones.
+ */
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR_FATAL_MASK					\
+		((1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_INIT_FAIL) |		\
+		 (1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_PLL_FAIL) |			\
+		 (1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_BINNING_FAIL) |		\
+		 (1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_SKIPPED) |		\
+		 (1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENG_ARC_MEM_SCRUB_FAIL) |	\
+		 (1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_EEPROM_FAIL))
+
+/*
+ * CPU error bits in BOOT_ERROR registers
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_DRAM_INIT_FAIL		DRAM initialization failed.
+ *					DRAM is not reliable to use.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_FIT_CORRUPTED		FIT data integrity verification of the
+ *					image provided by the host has failed.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_TS_INIT_FAIL		Thermal Sensor initialization failed.
+ *					Boot continues as usual, but keep in
+ *					mind this is a warning.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_DRAM_SKIPPED		DRAM initialization has been skipped.
+ *					Skipping DRAM initialization has been
+ *					requested (e.g. strap, command, etc.)
+ *					and FW skipped the DRAM initialization.
+ *					Host can initialize the DRAM.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_NIC_DATA_NOT_RDY	NIC data from BMC is not ready.
+ *					BMC has not provided the NIC data yet.
+ *					Once provided this bit will be cleared.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_NIC_FW_FAIL		NIC FW loading failed.
+ *					The NIC FW loading and initialization
+ *					failed. This means NICs are not usable.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_SECURITY_NOT_RDY	Chip security initialization has been
+ *					started, but is not ready yet - chip
+ *					cannot be accessed.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_SECURITY_FAIL		Security related tasks have failed.
+ *					The tasks are security init (root of
+ *					trust), boot authentication (chain of
+ *					trust), data packets authentication.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_EFUSE_FAIL		Reading from eFuse failed.
+ *					The PCI device ID might be wrong.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_PRI_IMG_VER_FAIL	Verification of primary image failed.
+ *					It mean that ppboot checksum
+ *					verification for the preboot primary
+ *					image has failed to match expected
+ *					checksum. Trying to program image again
+ *					might solve this.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_SEC_IMG_VER_FAIL	Verification of secondary image failed.
+ *					It mean that ppboot checksum
+ *					verification for the preboot secondary
+ *					image has failed to match expected
+ *					checksum. Trying to program image again
+ *					might solve this.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_PLL_FAIL		PLL settings failed, meaning that one
+ *					of the PLLs remains in REF_CLK
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_DEVICE_UNUSABLE_FAIL	Device is unusable and customer support
+ *					should be contacted.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_BOOT_FW_CRIT_ERR	Critical error was detected during
+ *					the execution of ppboot or preboot.
+ *					for example: stack overflow.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_BINNING_FAIL		Binning settings failed, meaning
+ *					malfunctioning components might still be
+ *					in use.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_TPM_FAIL		TPM verification flow failed.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR_EEPROM_FAIL		Failed reading EEPROM data. Defaults
+ *					are used.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENG_ARC_MEM_SCRUB_FAIL	Failed scrubbing the Engines/ARCFarm
+ *					memories. Boot disabled until reset.
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_ERR0_ENABLED		Error registers enabled.
+ *					This is a main indication that the
+ *					running FW populates the error
+ *					registers. Meaning the error bits are
+ *					not garbage, but actual error statuses.
+ */
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_DRAM_INIT_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_INIT_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_FIT_CORRUPTED		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_FIT_CORRUPTED)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_TS_INIT_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_TS_INIT_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_DRAM_SKIPPED		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_DRAM_SKIPPED)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_NIC_FW_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_NIC_FW_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_SECURITY_NOT_RDY		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_SECURITY_NOT_RDY)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_SECURITY_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_SECURITY_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_EFUSE_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_EFUSE_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_PRI_IMG_VER_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_PRI_IMG_VER_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_SEC_IMG_VER_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_SEC_IMG_VER_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_PLL_FAIL			(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_PLL_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_DEVICE_UNUSABLE_FAIL	(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_DEVICE_UNUSABLE_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_BOOT_FW_CRIT_ERR		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_BOOT_FW_CRIT_ERR)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_BINNING_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_BINNING_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_TPM_FAIL			(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_TPM_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_EEPROM_FAIL		(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_EEPROM_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_ENG_ARC_MEM_SCRUB_FAIL	(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENG_ARC_MEM_SCRUB_FAIL)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR0_ENABLED			(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENABLED)
+#define CPU_BOOT_ERR1_ENABLED			(1 << CPU_BOOT_ERR_ENABLED)
+
+enum cpu_boot_dev_sts {
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_SECURITY_EN = 0,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DEBUG_EN = 1,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_WATCHDOG_EN = 2,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DRAM_INIT_EN = 3,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DRAM_SCR_EN = 9,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_HARD_RST_EN = 10,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_LD_COM_EN = 16,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_IATU_CONF_EN = 17,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_ENABLED = 31,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_SCND_EN = 63,
+	CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_LAST = 64 /* we have 2 registers of 32 bits */
+};
+
+/*
+ * BOOT DEVICE STATUS bits in BOOT_DEVICE_STS registers
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_SECURITY_EN	Security is Enabled.
+ *					This is an indication for security
+ *					enabled in FW, which means that
+ *					all conditions for security are met:
+ *					device is indicated as security enabled,
+ *					registers are protected, and device
+ *					uses keys for image verification.
+ *					Initialized in: preboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_DEBUG_EN		Debug is enabled.
+ *					Enabled when JTAG or DEBUG is enabled
+ *					in FW.
+ *					Initialized in: preboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_WATCHDOG_EN	Watchdog is enabled.
+ *					Watchdog is enabled in FW.
+ *					Initialized in: preboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_DRAM_INIT_EN	DRAM initialization is enabled.
+ *					DRAM initialization has been done in FW.
+ *					Initialized in: Preboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_DRAM_SCR_EN	DRAM scrambler enabled.
+ *					FW initialized DRAM scrambler.
+ *					Initialized in: Preboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_FW_HARD_RST_EN	FW hard reset procedure is enabled.
+ *					FW has the hard reset procedure
+ *					implemented. This means that FW will
+ *					perform hard reset procedure on
+ *					receiving the halt-machine event.
+ *					Initialized in: preboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_FW_LD_COM_EN	Flexible FW loading communication
+ *					protocol is enabled.
+ *					Initialized in: preboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_FW_IATU_CONF_EN	FW iATU configuration is enabled.
+ *					This bit if set, means the iATU has been
+ *					configured and is ready for use.
+ *					Initialized in: ppboot
+ *
+ * CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_ENABLED		Device status register enabled.
+ *					This is a main indication that the
+ *					running FW populates the device status
+ *					register. Meaning the device status
+ *					bits are not garbage, but actual
+ *					statuses.
+ *					Initialized in: preboot
+ *
+ */
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_SECURITY_EN		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_SECURITY_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_DEBUG_EN		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DEBUG_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_WATCHDOG_EN		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_WATCHDOG_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_DRAM_INIT_EN		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DRAM_INIT_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_DRAM_SCR_EN		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_DRAM_SCR_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_FW_HARD_RST_EN	(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_HARD_RST_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_FW_LD_COM_EN		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_LD_COM_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_FW_IATU_CONF_EN	(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_FW_IATU_CONF_EN)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS0_ENABLED		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_ENABLED)
+#define CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS1_ENABLED		(1 << CPU_BOOT_DEV_STS_ENABLED)
+
+enum cpu_boot_status {
+	CPU_BOOT_STATUS_NA = 0,		/* Default value after reset of chip */
+	CPU_BOOT_STATUS_IN_WFE = 1,
+	CPU_BOOT_STATUS_DRAM_RDY = 2,

+	CPU_BOOT_STATUS_FW_SHUTDOWN_PREP = 18,

+/* TODO: remove the desc magic after the code is updated to use message */
+/* HCDM - Habana Communications Descriptor Magic */
+#define HL_COMMS_DESC_MAGIC	0x4843444D
+#define HL_COMMS_DESC_VER	1
+
+/* HCMv - Habana Communications Message + header version */
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALUE	0x48434D00
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_MASK		0xFFFFFF00
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VER_MASK	0xFF
+
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VER(ver)	(HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALUE |	\
+					((ver) & HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VER_MASK))
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_V0		HL_COMMS_DESC_MAGIC
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_V1		HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VER(1)
+
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC		HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_V1
+
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALIDATE_MAGIC(magic)			\
+		(((magic) & HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_MASK) ==			\
+		HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALUE)
+
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALIDATE_VERSION(magic, ver)			\
+		(((magic) & HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VER_MASK) >=		\
+		((ver) & HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VER_MASK))
+
+#define HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALIDATE(magic, ver)				\
+		(HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALIDATE_MAGIC((magic)) &&		\
+		HL_COMMS_MSG_MAGIC_VALIDATE_VERSION((magic), (ver)))
+
+enum comms_msg_type {
+	HL_COMMS_DESC_TYPE = 0,
+	HL_COMMS_RESET_CAUSE_TYPE = 1,
+	HL_COMMS_FW_CFG_SKIP_TYPE = 2,
+	HL_COMMS_MEM_ALLOC_TYPE = 3,
+	HL_COMMS_EEPROM_DEFAULTS_TYPE = 4,
+};

+/*
+ * Binning information shared between LKD and FW
+ * @tpc_mask_l - TPC binning information lower 64 bit
+ * @dec_mask - Decoder binning information
+ * @dram_mask - DRAM binning information
+ * @edma_mask - EDMA binning information
+ * @mme_mask_l - MME binning information lower 32
+ * @mme_mask_h - MME binning information upper 32
+ * @rot_mask - Rotator binning information
+ * @xbar_mask - xBAR binning information
+ * @reserved - reserved field for future binning info w/o ABI change
+ * @tpc_mask_h - TPC binning information upper 64 bit
+ * @nic_mask - NIC binning information
+ */
+struct lkd_fw_binning_info {
+	uint64_t nic_mask;
+	uint32_t dec_mask;
+	uint32_t dram_mask;
+	uint32_t rot_mask;
+	uint32_t reserved1[7];
+};
+
+/* this is the comms message header - meta data */
+struct comms_msg_header {
+	uint32_t magic;		/* magic for validation */
+	uint32_t crc32;		/* CRC32 of the message w/o header */
+	uint16_t size;		/* size of the message w/o header */
+	uint8_t version;	/* message payload version */
+	uint8_t type;		/* message type */
+	uint8_t reserved[4];	/* pad to 64 bit */
+};
+
+enum lkd_fw_ascii_msg_lvls {
+	LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_ERR = 0,
+	LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_WRN = 1,
+	LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_INF = 2,
+	LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_DBG = 3,
+};
+
+#define LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_MAX_LEN	128
+#define LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_MAX		4	/* consider ABI when changing */
+
+struct lkd_fw_ascii_msg {
+	uint8_t valid;
+	uint8_t msg_lvl;
+	uint8_t reserved[6];
+	char msg[LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_MAX_LEN];
+};

+enum comms_reset_cause {
+	HL_RESET_CAUSE_UNKNOWN = 0,
+	HL_RESET_CAUSE_HEARTBEAT = 1,
+	HL_RESET_CAUSE_TDR = 2,
+};
+
+enum comms_module_type {
+	MODULE_TYPE_MEZZ = 0,
+	MODULE_TYPE_PCI = 1,
+};
+
+struct comms_mem_alloc {
+	uint64_t virt_addr;
+	uint64_t phys_addr;
+	uint32_t size;
+	char reserved[4];
+};
+
+/* this is the comms message descriptor */
+struct lkd_fw_comms_msg {
+	struct comms_msg_header header;
+	/* union for future expantions of new messages */
+	union {
+		struct {
+			struct cpu_dyn_regs cpu_dyn_regs;
+			char fuse_ver[VERSION_MAX_LEN];
+			char cur_fw_ver[VERSION_MAX_LEN];
+			/* address for next FW component load */
+			uint64_t img_addr;
+			struct lkd_fw_binning_info binning_info;
+			struct lkd_fw_ascii_msg ascii_msg[LKD_FW_ASCII_MSG_MAX];
+			/* reserved memory size [MB] for FW/SVE */
+			uint32_t rsvd_mem_size_mb;
+			uint8_t module_type;
+			char reserved1[15];
+		};
+		struct {
+			uint8_t reset_cause;
+		};
+		struct {
+			uint8_t fw_cfg_skip; /* 1 - skip, 0 - don't skip */
+		};
+		struct { /* 1 - use defaults on err, 0 - fail boot on err */
+			uint8_t eeprom_defaults_on_err;
+		};
+		struct comms_mem_alloc alloc;
+	};
+};
+
+/*
+ * LKD commands:
+ *
+ * COMMS_NOOP			Used to clear the command register and no actual
+ *				command is send.
+ *
+ * COMMS_CLR_STS		Clear status command - FW should clear the
+ *				status register. Used for synchronization
+ *				between the commands as part of the race free
+ *				protocol.
+ *
+ * COMMS_RST_STATE		Reset the current communication state which is
+ *				kept by FW for proper responses.
+ *				Should be used in the beginning of the
+ *				communication cycle to clean any leftovers from
+ *				previous communication attempts.
+ *
+ * COMMS_PREP_DESC		Prepare descriptor for setting up the
+ *				communication and other dynamic data:
+ *				struct lkd_fw_comms_desc.
+ *				This command has a parameter stating the next FW
+ *				component size, so the FW can actually prepare a
+ *				space for it and in the status response provide
+ *				the descriptor offset. The Offset of the next FW
+ *				data component is a part of the descriptor
+ *				structure.
+ *
+ * COMMS_DATA_RDY		The FW data has been uploaded and is ready for
+ *				validation.
+ *
+ * COMMS_EXEC			Execute the next FW component.
+ *
+ * COMMS_RST_DEV		Reset the device.
+ *
+ * COMMS_GOTO_WFE		Execute WFE command. Allowed only on non-secure
+ *				devices.
+ *
+ * COMMS_SKIP_BMC		Perform actions required for BMC-less servers.
+ *				Do not wait for BMC response.
+ *
+ * COMMS_LOW_PLL_OPP		Initialize PLLs for low OPP.
+ *
+ * COMMS_PREP_DESC_ELBI		Same as COMMS_PREP_DESC only that the memory
+ *				space is allocated in a ELBI access only
+ *				address range.
+ *
+ */
+enum comms_cmd {
+	COMMS_NOOP = 0,
+	COMMS_CLR_STS = 1,
+	COMMS_RST_STATE = 2,
+	COMMS_PREP_DESC = 3,
+	COMMS_DATA_RDY = 4,
+	COMMS_EXEC = 5,
+	COMMS_RST_DEV = 6,
+	COMMS_GOTO_WFE = 7,
+	COMMS_SKIP_BMC = 8,
+	COMMS_LOW_PLL_OPP = 9,
+	COMMS_PREP_DESC_ELBI = 10,
+	COMMS_INVLD_LAST
+};
+
+#define COMMS_COMMAND_SIZE_SHIFT	0
+#define COMMS_COMMAND_SIZE_MASK		0x1FFFFFF
+#define COMMS_COMMAND_CMD_SHIFT		27
+#define COMMS_COMMAND_CMD_MASK		0xF8000000
+
+/*
+ * LKD command to FW register structure
+ * @size	- FW component size
+ * @cmd		- command from enum comms_cmd
+ */
+struct comms_command {
+	union {		/* bit fields are only for FW use */
+		struct {
+			uint32_t size :25;		/* 32MB max. */
+			uint32_t reserved :2;
+			enum comms_cmd cmd :5;		/* 32 commands */
+		};
+		uint32_t val;
+	};
+};
+
+/*
+ * FW status
+ *
+ * COMMS_STS_NOOP		Used to clear the status register and no actual
+ *				status is provided.
+ *
+ * COMMS_STS_ACK		Command has been received and recognized.
+ *
+ * COMMS_STS_OK			Command execution has finished successfully.
+ *
+ * COMMS_STS_ERR		Command execution was unsuccessful and resulted
+ *				in error.
+ *
+ * COMMS_STS_VALID_ERR		FW validation has failed.
+ *
+ * COMMS_STS_TIMEOUT_ERR	Command execution has timed out.
+ */
+enum comms_sts {
+	COMMS_STS_NOOP = 0,
+	COMMS_STS_ACK = 1,
+	COMMS_STS_OK = 2,
+	COMMS_STS_ERR = 3,
+	COMMS_STS_VALID_ERR = 4,
+	COMMS_STS_TIMEOUT_ERR = 5,
+	COMMS_STS_INVLD_LAST
+};
+
+/* RAM types for FW components loading - defines the base address */
+enum comms_ram_types {
+	COMMS_SRAM = 0,
+	COMMS_DRAM = 1,
+};
+
+#define COMMS_STATUS_OFFSET_SHIFT	0
+#define COMMS_STATUS_OFFSET_MASK	0x03FFFFFF
+#define COMMS_STATUS_OFFSET_ALIGN_SHIFT	2
+#define COMMS_STATUS_RAM_TYPE_SHIFT	26
+#define COMMS_STATUS_RAM_TYPE_MASK	0x0C000000
+#define COMMS_STATUS_STATUS_SHIFT	28
+#define COMMS_STATUS_STATUS_MASK	0xF0000000
+
+/*
+ * FW status to LKD register structure
+ * @offset	- an offset from the base of the ram_type shifted right by
+ *		  2 bits (always aligned to 32 bits).
+ *		  Allows a maximum addressable offset of 256MB from RAM base.
+ *		  Example: for real offset in RAM of 0x800000 (8MB), the value
+ *		  in offset field is (0x800000 >> 2) = 0x200000.
+ * @ram_type	- the RAM type that should be used for offset from
+ *		  enum comms_ram_types
+ * @status	- status from enum comms_sts
+ */
+struct comms_status {
+	union {		/* bit fields are only for FW use */
+		struct {
+			uint32_t offset :26;
+			enum comms_ram_types ram_type :2;
+			enum comms_sts status :4;	/* 16 statuses */
+		};
+		uint32_t val;
+	};
+};
+
+#define NAME_MAX_LEN	32 /* bytes */
+struct hl_module_data {
+	uint8_t name[NAME_MAX_LEN];
+	uint8_t version[VERSION_MAX_LEN];
+};
+
+/**
+ * struct hl_component_versions - versions associated with hl component.
+ * @struct_size: size of all the struct (including dynamic size of modules).
+ * @modules_offset: offset of the modules field in this struct.
+ * @component: version of the component itself.
+ * @fw_os: Firmware OS Version.
+ * @comp_name: Name of the component.
+ * @modules_counter: number of set bits in modules_mask.
+ * @reserved: reserved for future use.
+ * @modules: versions of the component's modules. Elborated explanation in
+ *              struct cpucp_versions.
+ */
+struct hl_component_versions {
+	uint16_t struct_size;
+	uint16_t modules_offset;
+	uint8_t component[VERSION_MAX_LEN];
+	uint8_t fw_os[VERSION_MAX_LEN];
+	uint8_t comp_name[NAME_MAX_LEN];
+	uint8_t modules_counter;
+	uint8_t reserved[3];
+	struct hl_module_data modules[];
+};
+
+/* Max size of fit size */
+#define HL_FW_VERSIONS_FIT_SIZE	4096
+
+#endif /* GPU_HL_BOOT_IF_H */

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier:     Apache-2.0

+ * All Rights Reserved.

+ */
+
+/* IMPORTANT:
+ * This file is needed for basic arcmgmt implementation.
+ * The filename and its contents was copied from the
+ * corresponding file under embedded/specs directory.
+ * Throughout the process of the arcmgmt implementation,
+ * it will be validated (i.e. discarded or modified).
+ * Refer to ticket FSW-6865.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef GPU_ENGINE_ARCS_IF_H
+#define GPU_ENGINE_ARCS_IF_H
+
+#define HL_ENGINE_ARC_ENGINE_ID_BITMASK		0xFF
+#define HL_ENGINE_ARC_INTR_TYPE_BITMASK		0xF
+#define HL_ENGINE_ARC_PAYLOAD_BITMASK		0xFFFFF
+
+#define HL_ENGINE_ARC_IRQ_ENGINE_ID(x)		((x) & HL_ENGINE_ARC_ENGINE_ID_BITMASK)
+#define HL_ENGINE_ARC_INTR_TYPE(x)		(((x) >> 8) & HL_ENGINE_ARC_INTR_TYPE_BITMASK)
+#define HL_ENGINE_ARC_QUEUE_INDEX(x)		(((x) >> 12) & HL_ENGINE_ARC_PAYLOAD_BITMASK)
+
+struct hl_engine_arc_irq_data {
+	/* ARC engine id e.g. DCORE0_TPC0_QM_ARC, DCORE0_TCP1_QM_ARC etc */
+	uint32_t engine_id:8;
+	/* Interrupt type e.g. DCCM_QUEUE_FULL */
+	uint32_t intr_type:4;
+	/* More info related to the interrupt e.g. queue index
+	 * incase of DCCM_QUEUE_FULL interrupt.
+	 */
+	uint32_t payload:20;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_HL_ENGINE_ARCS_IF_H */

+enum hl_fw_en_caps {

+
+#define HL_MEM_INFO_VERSION	1
+
+#define HL_NVME_MAX_ECC_SIZE	64
+#define HL_SRAM_ARRAYS_COUNT	3
+#define HL_HBM_ARRAYS_COUNT	3
+
+#define HL_NVME_MAX_PARITY_SIZE	64
+#define HL_PARITY_QUEUE_SIZE	(sizeof(uint32_t) + HL_NVME_MAX_PARITY_SIZE * sizeof(uint16_t))
+
+#define SRAM_ECC_QUEUE_WORDS	(HL_NVME_MAX_ECC_SIZE * HL_SRAM_ARRAYS_COUNT + 1)
+#define HBM_ECC_QUEUE_WORDS	(HL_NVME_MAX_ECC_SIZE * HL_HBM_ARRAYS_COUNT + 1)
+#define PARITY_QUEUE_WORDS	(HL_PARITY_QUEUE_SIZE / sizeof(uint32_t))
+
+struct hl_mem_hbm_bank_replacement_counters {
+	uint32_t hbm_banks_replaced_none;
+	uint32_t hbm_banks_replaced_low;
+	uint32_t hbm_banks_replaced_partial;
+	uint32_t hbm_banks_replaced_high;
+	uint32_t hbm_banks_replaced_max;
+};
+
+struct hl_mem_info_compat {
+	uint32_t version;
+	uint16_t sram_derr_count;
+	uint16_t sram_serr_count;
+	uint16_t sram_serr_threshold;
+	uint16_t hbm_derr_count;
+	uint16_t hbm_serr_count;
+	uint16_t hbm_serr_threshold;
+	uint8_t hbm_catastrophic_temp;
+	uint8_t rsvd[3];
+	uint32_t sram_serr_data[SRAM_ECC_QUEUE_WORDS];
+	uint32_t sram_derr_data[SRAM_ECC_QUEUE_WORDS];
+	uint32_t hbm_serr_data[HBM_ECC_QUEUE_WORDS];
+	uint32_t hbm_derr_data[HBM_ECC_QUEUE_WORDS];
+	uint32_t hbm_ca_parity[PARITY_QUEUE_WORDS];
+	uint32_t hbm_wr_parity[PARITY_QUEUE_WORDS];
+	uint32_t hbm_rd_parity[PARITY_QUEUE_WORDS];
+	uint16_t sram_derr_events;
+	uint16_t sram_fatal_events;
+	uint16_t sram_serr_bursts;
+	uint16_t hbm_derr_events;
+	uint16_t hbm_fatal_events;
+	uint16_t hbm_serr_bursts;
+	uint16_t hbm_fatal_parity;
+	struct hl_mem_hbm_bank_replacement_counters bank_replacement_counters;
+};
+
+#define HL_VUART_MAGIC_VER(v)	(0x86828400 | (v)) /* VRTx */
+#define HL_VUART_MAGIC		HL_VUART_MAGIC_VER(0)

+struct hl_vuart_queue {

+	uint32_t head;
+	uint32_t tail;
+	uint8_t data[];
+};

+struct hl_linux_pcr {

+};

+};

+/* Be able to address the whole SRAM */
+#define HL_UPD_STS_OFFSET_MASK	0xF8000000
+/* Be able to have FW up to 16MB */
+#define HL_UPD_CMD_SIZE_MASK	0xFF000000
+
+enum fw_post_reset_cmd {
+	FLOPS_UPD_NOOP = 0,
+	FLOPS_UPD_NXT_IMG1,
+	FLOPS_UPD_NXT_IMG2,

+	FLOPS_CPLD_UPDATED,
+	FLOPS_FUSE_PENDING,
+	FLOPS_EROM_UPDATED,
+};
+
+enum hl_upd_cmd {
+	HL_UPD_NOOP = 0,
+	HL_UPD_CLR_STS,
+	HL_UPD_RST_STATE,
+	HL_UPD_MAP_FLASH,
+	HL_UPD_MAP_EEPROM,
+	HL_UPD_PROG_FLASH,
+	HL_UPD_PROG_EEPROM,
+	HL_UPD_RST_DEV,
+	HL_UPD_MAP_CPLD,
+	HL_UPD_PROG_CPLD,
+	HL_UPD_MAP_SRAM,
+	HL_UPD_RUN_SRAM,
+	HL_UPD_GET_STATUS,
+	HL_UPD_MAP_ELBI,
+	HL_UPD_RUN_ELBI,
+	HL_UPD_GET_PROTO_STATUS,
+};
+
+enum hl_upd_sts {
+	HL_UPD_STS_NOOP = 0,
+	HL_UPD_STS_MAP_OK,
+	HL_UPD_STS_MAP_ERR,
+	HL_UPD_STS_MAP_ACK,
+	HL_UPD_STS_RUN_OK = 4,
+	HL_UPD_STS_PROG_OK = 4,
+	HL_UPD_STS_GET_STATUS_OK = 4,
+	HL_UPD_STS_OK = 4,
+	HL_UPD_STS_RUN_ERR = 5,
+	HL_UPD_STS_PROG_ERR = 5,
+	HL_UPD_STS_GET_STATUS_ERR = 5,
+	HL_UPD_STS_ERR = 5,
+	HL_UPD_STS_RUN_ACK = 6,
+	HL_UPD_STS_PROG_ACK = 6,
+	HL_UPD_STS_GET_STATUS_ACK = 6,
+	HL_UPD_STS_ACK = 6,
+	HL_UPD_STS_RST_OK,
+	HL_UPD_STS_RST_ACK,

+	HL_UPD_STS_TIMEOUT_ERR,
+	HL_UPD_STS_REPEAT,
+	HL_UPD_STS_INVLD_LAST
+};
+
+struct hl_upd_command {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint32_t size :24;
+			uint32_t reserved :4;
+			enum hl_upd_cmd cmd:4;
+		} __packed;
+		uint32_t val;
+	};
+};
+
+struct hl_upd_status {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint32_t offset :27;
+			uint32_t reserved :1;
+			enum hl_upd_sts status:4;
+		} __packed;
+		uint32_t val;
+	};
+};
+
+#define HL_UPD_PROTO_VERSION		1
+#define HL_UPD_PROTO_VERSION_INVALID	~0U
+
+struct hl_upd_proto_status {

+		} __packed;

+	};

+#define HL_I2C_EVENT_IS_PEC(event)		\
+	((event) == HL_STATUS_PEC ||		\
+	 (event) == HL_IDENTIFICATION_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_MODULE_ID_PEC ||		\
+	 (event) == HL_MODULE_STATUS_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_VERSIONS_PEC ||		\
+	 (event) == HL_MONITOR_PEC ||		\
+	 (event) == HL_ERROR_REPORT_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_FW_UPDATE_PEC ||		\
+	 (event) == HL_INDIRECT_REQ_STS_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_INDIRECT_REQ_CFG_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_INDIRECT_CTRL_CMD_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_INDIRECT_CTRL_STS_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_ERROR_STATUS_PEC ||	\
+	 (event) == HL_POL_PEC)
+
+ /* represent NVMe direct command 242 - 245 */
+struct hl_i2c_slave_error_status_struct {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			/* Byte 0 */
+			uint16_t fatal_err : 1;
+			uint16_t mem_err : 1;
+			uint16_t pcie_err : 1;
+			uint16_t nic_err : 1;
+			uint16_t temp_volt_err : 1;
+			uint16_t sec_err : 1;
+			uint16_t other_err: 1;
+			uint16_t reserve1 : 1;
+			/* Byte 1 */
+			uint16_t wd_err : 1;
+			uint16_t reserve3 : 1;
+			uint16_t chksum_err : 1;
+			uint16_t pll_lock_err : 1;
+			uint16_t reserve4 : 1;
+			uint16_t fit_err : 1;
+			uint16_t reserve2 : 2;
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t val8[2];
+		uint16_t val;
+	};
+} __packed;
+
+#endif /* GPU_I2C_SLAVE_EVENTS_H */

+
+#ifndef GPU_MBX_IF_H
+#define GPU_MBX_IF_H
+
+#include <stdint.h>
+#include "cpucp_if.h"
+
+#define MBX_MAX_PACKET_SIZE			4096
+
+enum hb_sender_id {
+	HB_SENDER_ARM = 1,
+	HB_SENDER_PID_ARC = 2,
+	HB_SENDER_EVT_ARC = 3
+};
+
+enum boot_info_sender_id {
+	BOOT_INFO_SENDER_NONE,
+	BOOT_INFO_SENDER_ARMCP,
+	BOOT_INFO_SENDER_UBOOT,
+	BOOT_INFO_SENDER_PID_ARC,
+	BOOT_INFO_SENDER_MAX
+};
+
+enum hl_mbx_msg_id {
+	HL_MBX_MSG_UNUSED,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_UNMASK_EVENT,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT0,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT1,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT2,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT3,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT4,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT5,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT6,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT7,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT8,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT9,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT10,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT11,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT12,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT13,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT14,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT15,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_PID_POWER_START_EVENT,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_PID_POWER_END_EVENT,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_PID_THERMAL_START_EVENT,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_PID_THERMAL_END_EVENT,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_HB_EVENT,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_PID_ZEPHYR_VERSION,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_PID_API,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_START_OPS,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_STOP_OPS,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_SHUTDOWN_OPS,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_PID_POWER_RDC_BRK,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_CHECK_ALIVE,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_SENSOR_SET,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_FREQ_SET,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_CPLD_SHUTDOWN,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_HARD_RESET,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_ECC_INJECTION,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_EVENT_DEFERRED,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_CPLD_I2C_RECOVERY,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_HBM_THROTTLING,
+	HL_MBX_MSG_ID_MAX
+};
+
+enum pid_api_rc {
+	PID_API_SUCCESS,
+	PID_API_INVALID_ARGUMENT,
+	PID_API_NOT_SUPPORTED,
+	PID_API_DATA_OUT_OF_RANGE,
+	PID_API_UNKNOWN_ERROR,
+};
+
+struct mbx_common_msg_id {
+	uint32_t msg_id:8; /* enum hl_mbx_msg_id */
+	uint32_t rsvd:24;
+};
+
+struct mbx_event_msg_id {
+	uint32_t msg_id:8; /* enum hl_mbx_msg_id */
+	uint32_t rsvd:23;
+	uint32_t is_fake:1;
+};
+
+/*
+ * The IPM message ID field is a 32-bit integer; the lower 8 bits are
+ * used to track the msg ID (enum hl_mbx_msg_id).  The remaining bits
+ * may be used by specific message IDs to define additional state/flags.
+ */
+struct mbx_msg_id {
+	union {
+		/* Valid for all message IDs */
+		struct mbx_common_msg_id common;
+
+		/* Valid only for EVENT0...EVENT15 and DEFERRED */
+		struct mbx_event_msg_id event;
+
+		uint32_t u32;
+	};
+};
+
+struct mbx_pid_api {
+	enum pid_api_rc ret;
+	union {
+		uint64_t cmd;
+		uint64_t resp;
+	};
+};
+
+struct mbx_packet_request {
+	uint32_t new_request;
+	uint8_t buf[MBX_MAX_PACKET_SIZE];
+};
+
+struct mbx_packet_response {
+	enum cpucp_packet_rc rc;
+	uint32_t new_response;
+};
+
+struct mbx_ver_response {
+	char str[VERSION_MAX_LEN];
+	uint32_t new_response;
+};
+
+struct mbx_linux_mem_response {
+	uint32_t mem_offset;
+	uint32_t mem_size;
+	uint32_t new_response;
+};
+
+struct mbx_arm_tx {
+	struct mbx_packet_response pkt_resp;
+	struct mbx_ver_response ver_resp;
+	struct mbx_linux_mem_response mem_resp;
+};
+
+struct mbx_arm_rx {
+	struct mbx_packet_request pkt_req;
+	uint32_t linux_ver_request;
+	uint32_t linux_mem_request;
+	uint32_t armcp_ver_request;
+	uint32_t uboot_ver_request;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_MBX_IF_H */

b/gpu/common/hl_pci_if.h

@@ -0,0 +1,103 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2023 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef GPU_HL_PCI_IF_H_
+#define GPU_HL_PCI_IF_H_
+
+/*
+ * enum hl_pci_bar_id - pci bar number enum
+ * since we are working in 64bit mode, only bars 0, 2, 4 are available.
+ */
+enum hl_pci_bar_id {
+	PCI_BAR_ID_0 = 0,
+	PCI_BAR_ID_2 = 2,
+	PCI_BAR_ID_4 = 4
+};
+
+/*
+ * enum hl_pci_match_mode - pci match mode per region
+ * @PCI_ADDRESS_MATCH_MODE: address match mode
+ * @PCI_BAR_MATCH_MODE: bar match mode
+ */
+enum hl_pci_match_mode {
+	PCI_ADDRESS_MATCH_MODE,
+	PCI_BAR_MATCH_MODE
+};
+
+/*
+ * BMU supports 2 types of regions, configuration region is translating
+ * addresses coming from BAR0, memory region is translating addresses coming
+ * from BAR4.
+ */
+enum hl_bmu_region_type {
+	BMU_REGION_TYPE_CFG,
+	BMU_REGION_TYPE_MEM
+};
+
+/*
+ * 8 configuration regions are supported by the BMU, and 4 memory regions.
+ * the memory and cfg regions are places in separate banks, so the region
+ * index is overlapping.
+ */
+enum hl_bmu_region_id {
+	BMU_REGION_0,
+	BMU_REGION_1,
+	BMU_REGION_2,
+	BMU_REGION_3,
+	BMU_MEM_REGION_MAX = BMU_REGION_3,
+	BMU_REGION_4,
+	BMU_REGION_5,
+	BMU_REGION_6,
+	BMU_REGION_7,
+	BMU_CFG_REGION_MAX = BMU_REGION_7,
+};
+
+/*
+ * struct hl_inbound_pci_region - inbound region descriptor
+ * @mode: pci match mode for this region
+ * @addr: region target address
+ * @size: region size in bytes
+ * @offset_in_bar: offset within bar (address match mode)
+ * @bar: bar id
+ */
+struct hl_pci_inbound_region {
+	enum hl_pci_match_mode mode;
+	uint64_t addr;
+	uint64_t size;
+	uint64_t offset_in_bar;
+	uint8_t bar;
+};
+
+/*
+ * struct hl_outbound_pci_region - outbound region descriptor
+ * @addr: region target address
+ * @size: region size in bytes
+ */
+struct hl_pci_outbound_region {
+	uint64_t addr;
+	uint64_t size;
+};
+
+struct hl_bmu_permissions {
+	uint64_t w_user;
+	uint64_t r_user;
+	uint8_t w_prot;
+	uint8_t r_prot;
+	uint8_t w_cache;
+	uint8_t r_cache;
+};
+
+struct hl_bmu_region {
+	uint64_t src_start;
+	uint64_t src_end;
+	uint64_t dst_base;
+	struct hl_bmu_permissions perms;
+	enum hl_bmu_region_type type;
+	enum hl_bmu_region_id id;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_HL_PCI_IF_H_ */

+
+/* IMPORTANT:
+ * This file is needed for basic arcmgmt implementation.
+ * The filename and its contents was copied from the
+ * corresponding file under embedded/specs directory.
+ * Throughout the process of the arcmgmt implementation,
+ * it will be validated (i.e. discarded or modified).
+ * Refer to ticket FSW-6865.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef GPU_PROFILER_TRACE_H
+#define GPU_PROFILER_TRACE_H
+
+#define PSOC_STM_TRACE_HIGH_PRIO_EVENTS	1
+#define PSOC_STM_TRACE_MED_PRIO_EVENTS	2
+#define PSOC_STM_TRACE_LOW_PRIO_EVENTS	3
+
+#define MAX_EVENTS_PER_CORE		(4096)
+#define MAX_HIGH_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE	(1024)
+#define MAX_MED_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE	(1024)
+#define MAX_LOW_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE	(2048)
+
+#define MED_PRIO_EVT_OFFSET		(MAX_HIGH_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE)
+#define LOW_PRIO_EVT_OFFSET		(MED_PRIO_EVT_OFFSET + MAX_MED_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE)
+
+#define PSOC_HIGH_PRIO_EVENT_IDX(evt)	(evt)
+#define PSOC_MED_PRIO_EVENT_IDX(evt)	(evt + MED_PRIO_EVT_OFFSET)
+#define PSOC_LOW_PRIO_EVENT_IDX(evt)	(evt + LOW_PRIO_EVT_OFFSET)
+
+enum psoc_evt_producer_id_t {
+	PSOC_ARC0,
+	PSOC_ARC1,
+	PSOC_ARM,     // Gaudi2
+	PSOC_ARC2     // Gaudi3
+};
+
+enum psoc_evt_type_toggle_values_t {
+	PSOC_EVENT_STARTED,
+	PSOC_EVENT_ENDED,
+	PSOC_TOGGLE_VALUES_MAX
+};
+
+enum psoc_profiler_evt_types_t {
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_NONE = 0,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_TOGGLE,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_STATUS,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_CPUCP_PACKET,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_VALUE,
+	/*
+	 * If a 64 bit value has to be logged on profiler, below two event
+	 * types can be used to trace two consecutive events with
+	 * upper 32-bit payload written first (PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_VALUE_HI)
+	 * followed by lower 32-bit value in next event (PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_VALUE_LO)
+	 */
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_VALUE_HI,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_TYPE_VALUE_LO,
+};
+
+enum psoc_profiler_evt_units_t {
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_UNIT_NONE,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_UNIT_MILLI_WATT,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_UNIT_MILLI_CELSIUS,
+	PSOC_PROFILER_EVENT_UNIT_PERCENT
+};
+
+enum arc_pid_high_prio_events_t {
+	ARC_PID_THERMAL_THROTTLING_START_SOC_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_THERMAL_THROTTLING_START_HBM_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_THERMAL_THROTTLING_END_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_POWER_THROTTLING_START_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_POWER_THROTTLING_END_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_POWER_HIGH_SPIKE_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_POWER_REGULATION_START_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_POWER_REGULATION_END_EVENT,
+	/* TODO: add all needed events here */
+	ARC_PID_HIGH_PRIO_MAX_EVENTS = MAX_HIGH_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE
+};
+
+enum arc_pid_med_prio_events_t {
+	ARC_PID_RATE_LIMIT_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_AVG_POWER_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_HOST_TDP_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_SOC_TEMP_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_SOC_LIMIT_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_HBM_TEMP_EVENT,
+	ARC_PID_HBM_LIMIT_EVENT,
+	/* TODO: add all needed events here */
+	ARC_PID_MED_PRIO_MAX_EVENTS = MAX_MED_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE
+};
+
+enum arc_pid_low_prio_events_t {
+	/* TODO: add all needed events here */
+	ARC_PID_LOW_PRIO_MAX_EVENTS = MAX_LOW_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE
+};
+
+enum arc_mgmt_high_prio_events_t {
+	/* TODO: add all needed events here */
+	ARC_MGMT_HIGH_PRIO_MAX_EVENTS = MAX_HIGH_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE
+};
+
+enum arc_mgmt_med_prio_events_t {
+	ARC_MGMT_CPUCP_PACKET_START_EVENT,
+	ARC_MGMT_CPUCP_PACKET_END_EVENT,
+	/* TODO: add all needed events here */
+	ARC_MGMT_MED_PRIO_MAX_EVENTS = MAX_MED_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE
+};
+
+enum arc_mgmt_low_prio_events_t {
+	/* TODO: add all needed events here */
+	ARC_MGMT_LOW_PRIO_MAX_EVENTS = MAX_LOW_PRIO_EVENTS_PER_CORE
+};
+
+struct profiler_evt_msg_and_type {
+	const char *msg;
+	enum psoc_profiler_evt_types_t evt_type;
+	enum psoc_profiler_evt_units_t data_units;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_PROFILER_TRACE_H */

b/gpu/common/hl_sec_if.h

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2016-2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef _HABANALABS_SEC_IF_H_
+#define _HABANALABS_SEC_IF_H_
+
+/* Number of security keys in rom */
+#define SEC_KEY_HASH_COUNT_IN_ROM		5
+#define SECURITY_DWORD_PER_KEY			12
+/*
+ * Public Key is key properties itself -
+ * R-sqyared + modulus + exponent + inverse + num bits.
+ * i.e. 384 + 384 + 8 + 4 + 4 = 784 bytes
+ */
+#define KEY_SIZE				784
+#define KEY_EXT_SIZE				0
+/* size of key properties in bytes */
+#define KEY_PROP_RR_SZ				384
+#define KEY_PROP_MOD_SZ				384
+#define KEY_PROP_EXP_SZ				8
+#define KEY_PROP_INV_SZ				4
+#define KEY_PROP_NB_SZ				4
+#define KEY_PROP_HDR_SZ				8
+
+/* 384 + 384 + 8 + 4 + 4 = 784 bytes */
+#define PUB_KEY_SZ				(KEY_PROP_RR_SZ + KEY_PROP_MOD_SZ + \
+						KEY_PROP_EXP_SZ + KEY_PROP_INV_SZ + \
+						KEY_PROP_NB_SZ)
+
+/* Maximum number of security versions that can be updated */
+#define SVN_MAX_VERSIONS			128
+/* EFUSE security version size in bytes */
+#define NO_OF_BITS_PER_BYTE			8
+#define SVN_EFUSE_SIZE				(SVN_MAX_VERSIONS / NO_OF_BITS_PER_BYTE)
+
+enum fuse_sec_state {
+	FUSE_SEC_UNKNWON = 0,
+	FUSE_SEC_EN = 1,
+	FUSE_SEC_DIS = 2,
+};
+
+struct hl_sec_status {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint32_t enabled:1;
+			uint32_t flash_wp:1;
+			uint32_t minimal_svn:8;
+			uint32_t num_of_keys:3;
+			uint32_t revoked_keys:8;
+			uint32_t eeprom_wp:1;
+			uint32_t mem_cleared:1;
+			uint32_t reserved:9;
+			uint32_t current_svn:8;
+			uint32_t current_key_idx:3;
+			uint32_t fuse_sec_en:2; /* fuse_sec_state */
+			uint32_t reserved2:19;
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t val[8];
+	};
+};
+
+#define MAX_NUM_OF_KEYS		5
+#define DWORDS_PER_KEY		12
+
+struct hl_fw_sec_info {
+	uint8_t sec_ver;	/* Minimum security version */
+	uint8_t key_revoke;	/* Key revokation bits */
+	uint8_t rma_enabled;	/* Rma enable bit */
+	uint8_t rsvd;		/* Reserved bytes for alignment */
+	uint32_t keys[MAX_NUM_OF_KEYS][DWORDS_PER_KEY];	/* Key hashes */
+	uint32_t efuse_sys_cfg;	/* Efuse security data at offset 0x3A */
+	uint32_t efuse_sys_cfg2;	/* Efuse security data at offset 0x3B */
+};
+
+enum mem_access_mode {
+	ACCESS_RO,
+	ACCESS_WR,
+	ACCESS_RW,
+};
+
+#endif /* _HABANALABS_SEC_IF_H_ */

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2022 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef HL_TPM2_H
+#define HL_TPM2_H
+
+
+#define TPM_MAX_ORDINAL			243
+#define TPM_MAX_PROTECTED_ORDINAL	12
+#define TPM_PROTECTED_ORDINAL_MASK	0xff
+#define TPM_CMD_COUNT_BYTE		2
+#define TPM_CMD_ORDINAL_BYTE		6
+/* Number of wait states to wait for */
+#define TPM_WAIT_STATES			100
+
+#define TPM_ACCESS(l)			(0x0000 | ((l) << 12))
+#define TPM_INT_ENABLE(l)               (0x0008 | ((l) << 12))
+#define TPM_STS(l)			(0x0018 | ((l) << 12))
+#define TPM_DATA_FIFO(l)		(0x0024 | ((l) << 12))
+#define TPM_DID_VID(l)			(0x0F00 | ((l) << 12))
+#define TPM_RID(l)			(0x0F04 | ((l) << 12))
+
+#define MAX_SPI_FRAMESIZE		32
+
+#define COMMAND_BUFFER_SIZE		256
+#define MAX_DIGEST_BUFFER		1024 /* TPM2B_MAX_BUFFER */
+
+/* To make strings of commands more easily */
+#define __MSB(x) 			((x) >> 8)
+#define __LSB(x)			((x) & 0xFF)
+#define tpm_u16(x)			__MSB(x), __LSB(x)
+#define tpm_u32(x)			tpm_u16((x) >> 16), tpm_u16((x) & 0xFFFF)
+
+#define TPM_HEADER_SIZE			10
+
+/* Max buffer size supported by our tpm */
+#define TPM_DEV_BUFSIZE			1260
+
+#define MS_TO_US(m)			((m) * 1000)
+
+#define min_t(type, x, y) ({			\
+	type __min1 = (x);			\
+	type __min2 = (y);			\
+	__min1 < __min2 ? __min1 : __min2; })
+
+enum tpm_duration {
+	TPM_SHORT = 0,
+	TPM_MEDIUM = 1,
+	TPM_LONG = 2,
+	TPM_UNDEFINED = 3,
+	TPM_DURATION_COUNT,
+};
+
+/**
+ * TPM2 Structure Tags for command/response buffers.
+ *
+ * @TPM2_ST_NO_SESSIONS: the command does not need an authentication.
+ * @TPM2_ST_SESSIONS: the command needs an authentication.
+ */
+enum tpm2_tag {
+	TPM2_ST_NO_SESSIONS	= 0x8001,
+	TPM2_ST_SESSIONS	= 0x8002,
+	TPM_ST_HASHCHECK	= 0x8024,
+};
+
+/**
+ * TPM2 command codes used at the beginning of a buffer, gives the command.
+ * @TPM2_CC_CLEAR: TPM2_Clear().
+ * @TPM2_CC_CLEARCONTROL: TPM2_ClearControl().
+ * @TPM2_CC_HIERCHANGEAUTH: TPM2_HierarchyChangeAuth().
+ * @TPM2_CC_PCR_SETAUTHPOL: TPM2_PCR_SetAuthPolicy().
+ * @TPM2_CC_DAM_RESET: TPM2_DictionaryAttackLockReset().
+ * @TPM2_CC_DAM_PARAMETERS: TPM2_DictionaryAttackParameters().
+ * @TPM2_CC_SEQUENCE_COMPLETE: TPM2_SequenceComplete().
+ * @TPM2_CC_STARTUP: TPM2_Startup().
+ * @TPM2_CC_SELF_TEST: TPM2_SelfTest().
+ * @TPM2_CC_SEQUENCE_UPDATE: TPM2_SequenceUpdate().
+ * @TPM2_CC_GET_CAPABILITY: TPM2_GetCapibility().
+ * @TPM2_CC_GET_RANDOM: TPM2_GetRandom().
+ * @TPM2_CC_PCR_READ: TPM2_PCR_Read().
+ * @TPM2_CC_PCR_EXTEND: TPM2_PCR_Extend().
+ * @TPM2_CC_PCR_SETAUTHVAL: TPM2_PCR_SetAuthValue().
+ * @TPM2_CC_HASH_SEQUENCE_START: TPM2_HashSequenceStart().
+ */
+enum tpm2_command_codes {
+	TPM2_CC_CLEAR			= 0x0126,
+	TPM2_CC_CLEARCONTROL		= 0x0127,
+	TPM2_CC_HIERCHANGEAUTH		= 0x0129,
+	TPM2_CC_PCR_ALLOCATE		= 0x012B,
+	TPM2_CC_PCR_SETAUTHPOL		= 0x012C,
+	TPM2_CC_DAM_RESET		= 0x0139,
+	TPM2_CC_DAM_PARAMETERS		= 0x013A,
+	TPM2_CC_SEQUENCE_COMPLETE	= 0x013E,
+	TPM2_CC_STARTUP			= 0x0144,
+	TPM2_CC_SELF_TEST		= 0x0143,
+	TPM2_CC_NV_WRITE		= 0x0137,
+	TPM2_CC_NV_READ			= 0x014E,
+	TPM2_CC_QUOTE			= 0x0158,
+	TPM2_CC_SEQUENCE_UPDATE		= 0x015C,
+	TPM2_CC_SIGN			= 0x015D,
+	TPM2_CC_READ_PUBLIC		= 0x0173,
+	TPM2_CC_GET_CAPABILITY		= 0x017A,
+	TPM2_CC_GET_RANDOM		= 0x017B,
+	TPM2_CC_HASH			= 0x017D,
+	TPM2_CC_PCR_READ		= 0x017E,
+	TPM2_CC_PCR_EXTEND		= 0x0182,
+	TPM2_CC_PCR_SETAUTHVAL		= 0x0183,
+	TPM2_CC_HASH_SEQUENCE_START	= 0x0186,
+};
+
+enum tpm2_timeout {
+	TPM_SHORT_TIMEOUT_US		= 10,
+	TPM_SHORT_TIMEOUT_MS		= 2,
+	TPM_LONG_TIMEOUT_MS		= 15,
+	TPM_TIMEOUT_MS			= 5,
+	SLEEP_DURATION_US		= 60,
+	SLEEP_DURATION_LONG_US		= 210,
+	TIS_SHORT_TIMEOUT_MS		= 750,
+	TIS_LONG_TIMEOUT_MS		= 2000,
+};
+
+enum tis_status {
+	TPM_STS_DATA_EXPECT		= 0x08,
+	TPM_STS_DATA_AVAIL		= 0x10,
+	TPM_STS_GO			= 0x20,
+	TPM_STS_COMMAND_READY		= 0x40,
+	TPM_STS_VALID			= 0x80,
+};
+
+enum tis_access {
+	TPM_ACCESS_REQUEST_USE		= 0x02,
+	TPM_ACCESS_REQUEST_PENDING	= 0x04,
+	TPM_ACCESS_ACTIVE_LOCALITY	= 0x20,
+	TPM_ACCESS_VALID		= 0x80,
+};
+
+/**
+ * TPM2 startup values.
+ *
+ * @TPM2_SU_CLEAR: reset the internal state.
+ * @TPM2_SU_STATE: restore saved state (if any).
+ */
+enum tpm2_startup_types {
+	TPM2_SU_CLEAR		= 0x0000,
+	TPM2_SU_STATE		= 0x0001,
+};
+
+/**
+ * TPM2 return codes.
+ */
+enum tpm2_return_codes {
+	TPM2_RC_SUCCESS		= 0x0000,
+	TPM2_RC_BAD_TAG		= 0x001E,
+	TPM2_RC_FMT1		= 0x0080,
+	TPM2_RC_HASH		= TPM2_RC_FMT1 + 0x0003,
+	TPM2_RC_VALUE		= TPM2_RC_FMT1 + 0x0004,
+	TPM2_RC_SIZE		= TPM2_RC_FMT1 + 0x0015,
+	TPM2_RC_BAD_AUTH	= TPM2_RC_FMT1 + 0x0022,
+	TPM2_RC_HANDLE		= TPM2_RC_FMT1 + 0x000B,
+	TPM2_RC_VER1		= 0x0100,
+	TPM2_RC_INITIALIZE	= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0000,
+	TPM2_RC_FAILURE		= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0001,
+	TPM2_RC_DISABLED	= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0020,
+	TPM2_RC_AUTH_MISSING	= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0025,
+	TPM2_RC_COMMAND_CODE	= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0043,
+	TPM2_RC_AUTHSIZE	= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0044,
+	TPM2_RC_AUTH_CONTEXT	= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0045,
+	TPM2_RC_NEEDS_TEST	= TPM2_RC_VER1 + 0x0053,
+	TPM2_RC_WARN		= 0x0900,
+	TPM2_RC_TESTING		= TPM2_RC_WARN + 0x000A,
+	TPM2_RC_REFERENCE_H0	= TPM2_RC_WARN + 0x0010,
+	TPM2_RC_LOCKOUT		= TPM2_RC_WARN + 0x0021,
+};
+
+/**
+ * TPM2 permanent handles.
+ *
+ * @TPM2_RH_OWNER: refers to the 'owner' hierarchy.
+ * @TPM2_RH_NULL: no hierarchy
+ * @TPM2_RS_PW: indicates a password.
+ * @TPM2_RH_LOCKOUT: refers to the 'lockout' hierarchy.
+ * @TPM2_RH_ENDORSEMENT: refers to the 'endorsement' hierarchy.
+ * @TPM2_RH_PLATFORM: refers to the 'platform' hierarchy.
+ */
+enum tpm2_handles {
+	TPM2_RH_OWNER		= 0x40000001,
+	TPM2_RH_NULL            = 0x40000007,
+	TPM2_RS_PW		= 0x40000009,
+	TPM2_RH_LOCKOUT		= 0x4000000A,
+	TPM2_RH_ENDORSEMENT	= 0x4000000B,
+	TPM2_RH_PLATFORM	= 0x4000000C,
+};
+
+/**
+ * TPM2 algorithms.
+ */
+enum tpm2_algorithms {
+	TPM2_ALG_SHA1           = 0x04,
+	TPM2_ALG_XOR            = 0x0A,
+	TPM2_ALG_SHA256         = 0x0B,
+	TPM2_ALG_SHA384         = 0x0C,
+	TPM2_ALG_SHA512         = 0x0D,
+	TPM2_ALG_NULL           = 0x10,
+	TPM2_ALG_SM3_256        = 0x12,
+};
+
+#endif /* HL_TPM2_H */

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.

+
+#ifndef GPU_NMS_IF_H
+#define GPU_NMS_IF_H
+
+#include <stdint.h>
+
+/**
+ * struct nms_psoc_desc - NMS and PSOC descriptor information
+ * @num_pcie_funcs: In Non-SRIOV mode, value = 1 (1PF)
+ *              In SRIOV mode, value = 2 (1PF + 1VF)
+ *              In SRIOV mode with 1PF + 4VF, NMS will be disabled
+ * @i2a_bridge_base_entry: Intel Interface 2 AXI bridge base entry
+ * @mem_type: Memory type, refer to enum TODO
+ * @pad: padding
+ * @base_addr_lo: Device stolen memory address lower 32 bits
+ * @base_addr_hi: Device stolen memory address upper 32 bits
+ * @size: Device stolen memory size
+ */
+struct nms_psoc_desc {
+	uint8_t num_pcie_funcs;
+	uint8_t i2a_bridge_base_entry;
+	uint8_t mem_type;
+	uint8_t pad;
+	uint32_t base_addr_lo;
+	uint32_t base_addr_hi;
+	uint32_t size;
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_NMS_IF_H */

b/gpu/common/pci_idekm.h

@@ -0,0 +1,139 @@
+/**
+ *  Copyright Notice:
+ *  Copyright 2021-2022 DMTF. All rights reserved.
+ *  License: BSD 3-Clause License. For full text see link: https://github.com/DMTF/libspdm/blob/main/LICENSE.md
+ **/
+
+/** @file
+ * Definitions of Integrity and data Encryption (IDE) ECN in PCI-SIG.
+ **/
+
+#ifndef PCI_IDE_KM_H
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_H
+
+
+#pragma pack(1)
+
+/* IDE_KM header*/
+
+typedef struct {
+    uint8_t object_id;
+} pci_ide_km_header_t;
+
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_OBJECT_ID_QUERY 0x00
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_OBJECT_ID_QUERY_RESP 0x01
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_OBJECT_ID_KEY_PROG 0x02
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_OBJECT_ID_KP_ACK 0x03
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_OBJECT_ID_K_SET_GO 0x04
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_OBJECT_ID_K_SET_STOP 0x05
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_OBJECT_ID_K_SET_GOSTOP_ACK 0x06
+
+
+/* IDE_KM QUERY*/
+
+typedef struct {
+    pci_ide_km_header_t header;
+    uint8_t reserved;
+    uint8_t port_index;
+} pci_ide_km_query_t;
+
+
+/* IDE_KM QUERY_RESP*/
+
+typedef struct {
+    pci_ide_km_header_t header;
+    uint8_t reserved;
+    uint8_t port_index;
+    uint8_t dev_func_num;
+    uint8_t bus_num;
+    uint8_t segment;
+    uint8_t max_port_index;
+    /*IDE Extended capability*/
+} pci_ide_km_query_resp_t;
+
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_LINK_IDE_REG_BLOCK_MAX_COUNT 8
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_SELECTIVE_IDE_REG_BLOCK_MAX_COUNT 255
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_SELECTIVE_IDE_ADDRESS_ASSOCIATION_REG_BLOCK_MAX_COUNT 15
+
+
+/* IDE_KM KEY_PROG*/
+
+typedef struct {
+    pci_ide_km_header_t header;
+    uint8_t reserved[2];
+    uint8_t stream_id;
+    uint8_t reserved2;
+    uint8_t key_sub_stream;
+    uint8_t port_index;
+    /*KEY 8 DW
+     * IFV(invocation field of the IV) 2 DW*/
+} pci_ide_km_key_prog_t;
+
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_SET_MASK 0x01
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_SET_K0 0x00
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_SET_K1 0x01
+
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_DIRECTION_MASK 0x02
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_DIRECTION_RX 0x00
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_DIRECTION_TX 0x02
+
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_SUB_STREAM_MASK 0xF0
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_SUB_STREAM_PR 0x00
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_SUB_STREAM_NPR 0x10
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KEY_SUB_STREAM_CPL 0x20
+
+/* IDE_KM KP_ACK*/
+
+typedef struct {
+    pci_ide_km_header_t header;
+    uint8_t reserved[2];
+    uint8_t stream_id;
+    uint8_t status;
+    uint8_t key_sub_stream;
+    uint8_t port_index;
+} pci_ide_km_kp_ack_t;
+
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KP_ACK_STATUS_SUCCESS 0x00
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KP_ACK_STATUS_INCORRECT_LENGTH 0x01
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KP_ACK_STATUS_UNSUPPORTED_PORT_INDEX 0x02
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KP_ACK_STATUS_UNSUPPORTED_VALUE 0x03
+#define PCI_IDE_KM_KP_ACK_STATUS_UNSPECIFIED_FAILURE 0x04
+
+/* IDE_KM K_SET_GO*/
+
+typedef struct {
+    pci_ide_km_header_t header;

+} pci_ide_km_k_set_go_t;

b/gpu/common/queue_proto_d2h_async_if.h

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef GPU_QUEUE_PROTO_D2H_ASYNC_IF_H
+#define GPU_QUEUE_PROTO_D2H_ASYNC_IF_H
+
+#include <stdint.h>
+
+/**
+ * struct queue_proto_d2h_async_event - Device to Host async event
+ * @size: size of packet in bytes including control, header and payload[]
+ * 	and must be 4-byte-size-alignment.
+ * @index: Index sent by FW for driver to check for spurious/unordered
+ * 	events. The firmware writes the index in each entry and the driver
+ * 	verifies that the index of the current entry is larger by 1 of the
+ * 	index of the previous entry.
+ * @global_code: Global code used by different FW type implementation
+ * 	For PSOC FW implemenation of global code, refer to PSOC FW specific interface file
+ * 	For MUC FW implementation of global code, refer to MUC FW specific interface file
+ * @payload: Array of payload data - can be NULL depending on async event type
+ */
+struct queue_proto_d2h_async_event {
+	struct {
+		uint16_t size;
+		uint16_t index;
+	};
+
+	uint32_t global_code;
+
+	uint32_t payload[];
+};
+
+#endif /* GPU_QUEUE_PROTO_D2H_ASYNC_IF_H */

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2024 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+#ifndef _HL_RMA_IF_H_
+#define _HL_RMA_IF_H_
+
+#define RMA_VERSION	1
+
+#define HBM_GLOBAL_ECC_COUNTERS		8
+#define HBM_COUNTERS_PER_ENTRY		2
+#define NUM_OF_HBM_ECC_COUNTER_ENTRIES	(HBM_GLOBAL_ECC_COUNTERS / HBM_COUNTERS_PER_ENTRY)
+
+enum rma_tables_type {
+	RMA_HEADER_TABLE,
+	RMA_HBM_ECC_TABLE,
+	RMA_HBM_PARITY_TABLE,
+	RMA_HBM_ROW_REPLACEMENT_TABLE,
+	RMA_HBM_PAGE_DISCARD_TABLE,
+	RMA_HBM_LANE_REPAIR_TABLE,
+	RMA_HBM_SPARE_ROWS_TABLE,
+	RMA_NUM_OF_TABLES
+};
+
+enum hbm_init_sts {
+	HBM_NO_FAIL,
+	HBM_PLL_GET_FAIL,
+	HBM_PHY_INIT_FAIL,
+	HBM_MRS_OR_RST_FAIL,
+	HBM_PRE_TRAINING_FAIL,
+	HBM_MCBIST_FAIL,
+	HBM_TRAINING_FAIL,
+	HBM_MBIST_FAIL,
+	HBM_KNOWN_LANE_REPAIR_FAIL,
+	HBM_KNOWN_ROW_REPAIR_FAIL,
+	HBM_FUSE_SCAN_FAIL,
+};
+
+struct hbm_ecc_counters {
+	uint8_t hbm_ecc_counter1 : 4;
+	uint8_t hbm_ecc_counter2 : 4;
+} __packed;
+
+struct table_header {
+	uint32_t version;
+	uint32_t entry_size;
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t sram_ecc_counter;
+			uint8_t reserved2[3];
+		} __packed;
+		struct { /* temporary before removal */
+			uint8_t hbm_ecc_counter;
+			uint8_t reserved3[3];
+		} __packed;
+		struct {
+			struct hbm_ecc_counters hbm_ecc_counters[NUM_OF_HBM_ECC_COUNTER_ENTRIES];
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t reserved[4];
+	};
+	uint16_t hdr_sz;
+	uint8_t reserved4[2];
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_failure {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t sram_ecc : 1;
+			uint8_t hbm_ecc : 1;
+			uint8_t hbm_parity : 1;
+			uint8_t row_replacement : 1;
+			uint8_t hbm_init : 1;
+			uint8_t hbm_lane_repair : 1;
+			uint8_t sram_repair : 1;
+			uint8_t hbm_mbist_row_repair : 1; /* Only when unrepairable mbist errors */
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t data;
+	};
+} __packed;
+
+struct hbm_init_err {
+	uint8_t data; /* enum hbm_init_sts */
+} __packed;
+
+struct hbm_row_repair_err {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t no_spare_row_in_bank : 1;
+			uint8_t row_rep_table_full : 1;
+			uint8_t row_rep_done_previously : 1;
+			uint8_t ecc_table_full : 1;
+			uint8_t p1500_err : 1;
+			uint8_t scrubbing_failed : 1;
+			uint8_t reserved : 1;
+			uint8_t err_during_boot_or_margin_tool : 1; /* 0: boot, 1: margin_tool */
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t data;
+	};
+} __packed;
+
+struct hbm_mbist_err {
+	uint8_t hbm_idx;
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t pll_lock_fail : 1;
+			uint8_t p1500_err : 1;
+			uint8_t hbm_reset_fail : 1;
+			uint8_t mbist_busy : 1;
+			uint8_t internal_fault : 1;
+			uint8_t reserved : 2;
+			uint8_t err_during_boot_or_margin_tool : 1; /* 0: boot, 1: margin_tool */
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t data;
+	};
+} __packed;
+
+struct entry_header {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t validity : 2;
+			uint8_t count : 5;
+			uint8_t reserved : 1;
+		} __packed;
+		struct { /* Temporary before removal */
+			uint8_t validity2 : 2;
+			uint8_t is_multiple : 1;
+			uint8_t reserved4 : 5;
+		} __packed;
+	};
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t serr_cause : 1;
+			uint8_t derr_cause : 1;
+			uint8_t mbist_cause : 1;
+			uint8_t faked_ecc : 1;
+			uint8_t reserved2 : 4;
+		} __packed;
+		struct {
+			uint8_t ca_cause : 1;
+			uint8_t rd_cause : 1;
+			uint8_t wr_cause : 1;
+			uint8_t faked_par : 1;
+			uint8_t reserved3 : 4;
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t data_cause;
+	};
+} __packed;
+
+struct hbm_ecc_err {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint8_t row_rep_cause_lo : 1; /* row_repair_cause_lo */
+			uint8_t row_rep_index : 5;
+			uint8_t row_rep_cause_hi : 2; /* row_repair_cause_hi */
+		} __packed;
+		uint8_t data;
+	};
+} __packed;
+
+struct hbm_lane_repair_err {
+	uint8_t cause; // enum hbm_lane_repair_cause
+	uint8_t hbm_num;
+	uint8_t mc_pc;
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_entry {
+	struct entry_header header;
+	uint8_t version;
+	struct rma_failure rma_failure;
+	struct hbm_init_err hbm_init_err;
+	struct hbm_ecc_err hbm_ecc_err;
+	struct hbm_lane_repair_err hbm_lane_repair_err;
+	struct hbm_row_repair_err hbm_row_repair_err;
+	struct hbm_mbist_err hbm_mbist_err; /* populated only when hbm init err is due to mbist */
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_hbm_ecc_entry {
+	struct entry_header header;
+	uint8_t device : 4;
+	uint8_t pc : 4;
+	uint8_t sid : 2;
+	uint8_t bank : 6;
+	uint32_t row : 15;
+	uint32_t addr: 10;
+	uint32_t reserved : 7;
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_hbm_row_replacement_entry {
+	struct entry_header header;
+	uint8_t device : 4;
+	uint8_t pc : 4;
+	uint8_t sid : 2;
+	uint8_t bank : 6;
+	uint32_t row : 15;
+	uint32_t addr: 10;
+	uint32_t repair_type : 1; /* soft_repair = 0, hard_repair = 1*/
+	uint32_t reserved : 6;
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_hbm_spare_row_entry {
+	uint8_t spare_rows;
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_sram_ecc_entry {
+	struct entry_header header;
+	uint16_t block_index;
+	uint32_t address;
+	uint8_t wrapper_idx;
+	uint8_t reserved0;
+	uint16_t reserved1;
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_hbm_parity_entry {
+	struct entry_header header;
+	uint8_t device : 4;
+	uint8_t pc : 4;
+	uint8_t reserved;
+} __packed;
+
+struct rma_hbm_lane_repair_entry {
+	struct entry_header header;
+	uint8_t hbm_num;
+	uint8_t mc_pc;
+	uint32_t repair_code;
+} __packed;
+
+#endif /* _HL_RMA_IF_H_ */
