+
+struct mem_copy_flag {
+	uint8_t copy_type;
+	uint8_t mem_type;
+};
+
+enum dma_error {
+	DMA_SUCCESS = 0,
+	DMA_INVALID_CRC_ERROR,
+	DMA_INVALID_DEST_TYPE,
+	DMA_UNALIGNED_IMG_SIZE,
+	DMA_UNALIGNED_DEST_ADDR,
+	DMA_INVALID_IMG_SIZE,
+	DMA_ERROR_MAX
+};
+
+int boot_rom_mem_init(uint64_t addr, uint32_t size) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int boot_rom_mem_copy(struct mem_copy_flag flag, uint64_t src, uint64_t dst,
+			uint32_t size)__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+uint32_t boot_rom_fw_mem_arc_addr(uint64_t addr) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+/**
+ * @brief perform spi dma
+ *
+ * This API does spi dma from spi to fw mem or hbw memory
+ *
+ * @param image_size size of the image to be dma from spi
+ * @param flash_offset start offset of image in flash
+ * @param dest_addr fw mem or hbw address where image should be copied
+ * @param skip_crc_check api will skip crc check when true
+ * @param manual_crc api will perform manual crc check at the end of dma when true
+ * @param expected_crc 32-bit crc value to compare with at the end of dma. Valid
+ * only if manual_crc is true.
+ */
+enum dma_error boot_rom_spi_dma(uint32_t image_size, uint32_t flash_offset,
+				uint64_t dest_addr, bool skip_crc_check,
+				bool manual_crc, uint32_t expected_crc)__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int secure_boot_verify_image_hash384(uint8_t *exp_hash, unsigned char *img,
+				uint32_t img_len) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int lfs_file_set(const char *file_tag, uint32_t type, const void *data, uint32_t size)
+		__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int lfs_file_copy(const char *file_tag, uint32_t type, void *desc_data, uint32_t size)
+		__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int lfs_file_copy_partial(const char *file_tag, uint32_t type, void *data,
+			uint32_t offset, uint32_t size) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+

+ * 1 -> Initial version
+ *      hl_fw_boot_ptrs:
+ *              > boot_ptrs_img_sz : Size of the complete manifest to dma.
+ *              > img_desc_sz : Size of a single image descriptor.
+ *              > hl_fw_boot_magic : Packed with ['N','X','T', hl_fw_boot_ptr_imgs]
+ *              > count : Image descriptors count
+ *              > pub_key_sz : public key size
+ *              > sig_sz : signature size
+ *              > sec_ver : security version
+ *              > key_idx : key index to be validated
+ *              > offset2array : offset to array of offsets
+ *      hl_fw_img_desc:
+ *              > hl_fw_boot_magic : Packed with ['P','T','R', hl_fw_img_type]
+ *              > desc_ver : descriptor version
+ *              > idx : index used to identify primary and secondary image
+ *              > offset[2] : Image offset on SPI
+ *              > size[2] : Image size array
+ *              > load_high[2] : high [32-63] bits of the load address
+ *              > load_low[2] : low [0-31] bits of the load address
+ *              > chksum[2] : sha384 checksum
+ *              > start_high[2] : high [32-63] bits of the start address
+ *              > start_low[2] : low [0-31] bits of the start address
+ */
+
+#define HL_FW_IMG_DESC_CUR_VER	1
+
+#endif

b/gpu/fs1/fs1_fw_if.h

@@ -0,0 +1,151 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2019-2023 HabanaLabs, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef FS1_FW_IF_H
+#define FS1_FW_IF_H
+
+/* rst src bit in PSOC_GLOBAL_CONF_RST_SRC register */
+enum fs1_rst_src_bit {
+	HL_COLD_RST,
+	HL_MANUAL_RST,
+	HL_PRSTN_RST,
+	HL_SOFT_RST,
+	HL_WD_RST,
+	HL_FW_ALL_RST,
+	HL_SW_ALL_RST,
+	HL_FLR_RST,
+	HL_ECC_DERR_RST,
+	HL_CXL_RST,
+	HL_HOT_RST,
+	HL_VF_FLR_RST,
+	HL_UNKNOWN_RST = 255
+};
+
+/*
+ * CPU ID must be written before the CPU reset is released. Since ARC MNG is the first
+ * to run (by HW), its ID will be the default (0). For the other CPUs a unique ID
+ * is set.
+ */
+#define IO0_PSOC_ARC_MNG_ID		0
+#define IO0_PSOC_ARC_RST_ID		1
+#define IO0_PSOC_ARC_PID_ID		2
+#define IO0_PSOC_ARC_MISC_ID		3
+#define IO0_PCIE_ARC_CXL_ID		4
+
+struct fs1_cold_rst_data {
+	union {
+		struct {
+			uint32_t recovery_flag: 1;
+			uint32_t validation_flag: 1;
+			uint32_t valid_manifest_idx: 1;
+			uint32_t efuse_read_flag: 1;
+			uint32_t spsram_init_done : 1;
+			uint32_t arc1_iccm_init_done: 1;
+			uint32_t tpm_validation_flag: 1;
+			uint32_t arc1_persist_dccm_init_done: 1;
+			uint32_t fuse_emulation_init_done: 1;
+			uint32_t boot_info_set_done: 1;
+			uint32_t reserved : 22;
+		};
+		uint32_t data;
+	};
+};
+
+#define FS1_NUM_HALF_DIES		2
+#define FS1_NUM_IO_DIES		2
+#define FS1_NUM_COMPUTE_DIES		4
+#define FS1_NUM_REDUNDANT_EDMA		2
+
+#define MAX_DIE_COUNT		(FS1_NUM_IO_DIES + FS1_NUM_COMPUTE_DIES)
+
+/* In case there is a defect, io die redundant components are
+ * 1 of 9 DEC in each IO Die half
+ * 1 of 13 NICs in each IO Die
+ * PCIE_CLS in IO Die 1
+ * PMMU in IO Die for production (only used in IO Die stand-alone package)
+ * redundancy structure for FS1 IO die
+ */
+struct fs1_redundancy_ctx_io {
+	uint8_t initialized;		/* track driver initialization */
+	uint16_t dec_fault_mask[FS1_NUM_HALF_DIES];
+	uint16_t nic_fault_mask;
+	uint8_t pmmu_fault_mask;
+	uint8_t pcie_cls_fault_mask;	/* used only for die 1 */
+
+	int8_t redundant_dec[FS1_NUM_HALF_DIES];
+	int8_t redundant_nic;
+	int8_t redundant_pmmu;
+	int8_t redundant_die_pcie_cls;	/* used only for die 1 */
+};
+
+/* compute die redundant components are
+ * 2 of 5 EDMAs in each Compute Die
+ * 1 of 3 PDMAs in each Compute Die
+ * 1 of every 17 XeCores in Compute Die
+ * 1 CS + 6x HBM PC , AKA Dual plane.
+ * redundancy structure for FS1 compute die
+ */
+struct fs1_redundancy_ctx_compute {
+	uint8_t initialized;		/* track driver initialization */
+	uint8_t edma_fault_mask;
+	uint8_t pdma_fault_mask;
+	uint32_t xecore_fault_mask;
+
+	/* TODO  Dual Plane */
+	int8_t redundant_edma[FS1_NUM_REDUNDANT_EDMA];
+	int8_t redundant_pdma;
+	int8_t redundant_xecore;
+};
+
+/* redundancy structure for FS1 */
+struct fs1_redundancy_ctx {
+	struct fs1_redundancy_ctx_io io_die[FS1_NUM_IO_DIES];
+	struct fs1_redundancy_ctx_compute compute_die[FS1_NUM_COMPUTE_DIES];
+};
+
+/*
+ * Note:
+ * IO Half1 Die ID's are Virtual, just used for register offsets.
+ * Actual die ids are same between half0 and half1.
+ */
+enum fs1_die_id {
+	FS1_COMPUTE_DIE_0 = 0,
+	FS1_COMPUTE_DIE_1 = 1,
+	FS1_COMPUTE_DIE_2 = 2,
+	FS1_COMPUTE_DIE_3 = 3,
+	FS1_IO_DIE_0 = 4,
+	FS1_IO_DIE_0_HALF_1 = 5,	/* Virtual Die ID */
+	FS1_IO_DIE_1 = 6,
+	FS1_IO_DIE_1_HALF_1 = 7,	/* Virtual Die ID */
+	FS1_INVALID_DIE_ID = 8,
+};
+
+enum fs1_die_type {
+	FS1_COMPUTE_DIE,
+	FS1_IO_DIE_MASTER,
+	FS1_IO_DIE_SLAVE,
+	FS1_INVALID_DIE_TYPE,
+};
+
+enum fs1_sku_type {
+	FS1_FULL_PACKAGE,
+	FS1_PCIE_CARD,
+	FS1_IO_DIE_SA,
+	FS1_INVALID_SKU,
+};
+
+/* This enum used as index while fake fuse bin creation and to retrieve */
+enum fake_fuse_bin_index {
+ FAKE_FUSE_IO_D0,
+ FAKE_FUSE_IO_D1,
+ FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D0,
+ FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D1,
+ FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D2,
+ FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D3,
+};
+
+#endif /* FS1_FW_IF_H */

+#define SPI_FLASH_REGION_RESERVED_2_OFFSET			350000
+#define SPI_FLASH_REGION_RESERVED_2_SIZE			b0000
+
+/*
+ * FS1 Dup engine supports two regions with 8 groups each. Region 0
+ * On IO die, Region 0 is allocated to mgmt arc, Region 1 is
+ * allocated to pid Arc.
+ * On Compute die, Region 0 is allocated to mgmt arc, Region 1 is
+ * not used
+ * DUP_GROUP_MMIO_RANGE = 512, DUP_NUM_GROUPS_PER_REGION =8
+ */
+#define DUP_ENGINE_IO_REGION_0_OFFSET		f9cfc000
+#define DUP_ENGINE_IO_REGION_1_OFFSET		f9cfd000
+

b/gpu/jgs/boot_rom_export_dev.h

@@ -0,0 +1,62 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier:     GPL-2.0+
+ *
+ * Copyright (C) 2025 Intel Ltd.
+ */
+
+#ifndef SPECS_JGS_BOOT_ROM_EXPORT_DEV_H_
+#define SPECS_JGS_BOOT_ROM_EXPORT_DEV_H_
+
+struct mem_copy_flag {
+	uint8_t copy_type;
+	uint8_t mem_type;
+};
+
+enum dma_error {
+	DMA_SUCCESS = 0,
+	DMA_INVALID_CRC_ERROR,
+	DMA_INVALID_DEST_TYPE,
+	DMA_UNALIGNED_IMG_SIZE,
+	DMA_UNALIGNED_DEST_ADDR,
+	DMA_INVALID_IMG_SIZE,
+	DMA_ERROR_MAX
+};
+
+int boot_rom_mem_init(uint64_t addr, uint32_t size) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int boot_rom_mem_copy(struct mem_copy_flag flag, uint64_t src, uint64_t dst,
+			uint32_t size)__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+uint32_t boot_rom_fw_mem_arc_addr(uint64_t addr) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+/**
+ * @brief perform spi dma
+ *
+ * This API does spi dma from spi to fw mem or hbw memory
+ *
+ * @param image_size size of the image to be dma from spi
+ * @param flash_offset start offset of image in flash
+ * @param dest_addr fw mem or hbw address where image should be copied
+ * @param skip_crc_check api will skip crc check when true
+ * @param manual_crc api will perform manual crc check at the end of dma when true
+ * @param expected_crc 32-bit crc value to compare with at the end of dma. Valid
+ * only if manual_crc is true.
+ */
+enum dma_error boot_rom_spi_dma(uint32_t image_size, uint32_t flash_offset,
+				uint64_t dest_addr, bool skip_crc_check,
+				bool manual_crc, uint32_t expected_crc)__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int secure_boot_verify_image_hash384(uint8_t *exp_hash, unsigned char *img,
+				uint32_t img_len) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int lfs_file_set(const char *file_tag, uint32_t type, const void *data, uint32_t size)
+		__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int lfs_file_copy(const char *file_tag, uint32_t type, void *desc_data, uint32_t size)
+		__attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+int lfs_file_copy_partial(const char *file_tag, uint32_t type, void *data,
+			uint32_t offset, uint32_t size) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+uint32_t lfs_disk_set_empty(void) __attribute__ ((long_call));
+
+#endif /* SPECS_JGS_BOOT_ROM_EXPORT_DEV_H_ */

b/gpu/jgs/jgs_boot_info.h

@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2025 Intel, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+ #ifndef JGS_BOOT_INFO_H
+ #define JGS_BOOT_INFO_H
+
+ #define HL_BOOT_INFO_VERSION	0x1
+
+ /*
+  * Descriptor version change log:
+  * 1 -> Initial version
+  *      hl_fw_boot_ptrs:
+  *              > boot_ptrs_img_sz : Size of the complete manifest to dma.
+  *              > img_desc_sz : Size of a single image descriptor.
+  *              > hl_fw_boot_magic : Packed with ['N','X','T', hl_fw_boot_ptr_imgs]
+  *              > count : Image descriptors count
+  *              > pub_key_sz : public key size
+  *              > sig_sz : signature size
+  *              > sec_ver : security version
+  *              > key_idx : key index to be validated
+  *              > offset2array : offset to array of offsets
+  *      hl_fw_img_desc:
+  *              > hl_fw_boot_magic : Packed with ['P','T','R', hl_fw_img_type]
+  *              > desc_ver : descriptor version
+  *              > idx : index used to identify primary and secondary image
+  *              > offset[2] : Image offset on SPI
+  *              > size[2] : Image size array
+  *              > load_high[2] : high [32-63] bits of the load address
+  *              > load_low[2] : low [0-31] bits of the load address
+  *              > chksum[2] : sha384 checksum
+  *              > start_high[2] : high [32-63] bits of the start address
+  *              > start_low[2] : low [0-31] bits of the start address
+  */
+
+ #define HL_FW_IMG_DESC_CUR_VER	1
+
+ #endif

+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+ *
+ * Copyright 2025 Intel, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ */
+
+ #ifndef _SPECS_JGS_EFUSE_H_
+ #define _SPECS_JGS_EFUSE_H_
+
+ #define IO_EFUSE_SYSCFG_OFFSET		60
+ #define IO_EFUSE_SYSCFG2_OFFSET		61
+ #define IO_EFUSE_SVN_FW_OFFSET		63
+
+ #define IO_EFUSE_SVN_FW_DWORD_COUNT	4
+
+ #define EFUSE_WORDS_PER_BANK		128
+
+ /* runtime settings for PALAD use only (using spare address in IO fuse) */
+ #define IO_EFUSE_PALAD_CFG_0_OFFSET	47
+ #define SRIOV_NUM_OF_VFS_M		GENMASK(2, 0) /* number of SRIOV virtual functions */
+ #define ATS_CAPABILITY_ENABLED_M	BIT(3)	/* enable PCI ATS capability */
+ #define CXL_CACHE_CAP_ENABLED_M		BIT(4)	/* enable CXL.Cache capability */
+ #define BLOADER_COPY_LARGE_APP		BIT(5)	/* app is bigger than single ICCM memory */
+ #define BLOADER_COPY_BOOTINFO		BIT(6)	/* init bootinfo section in spsram from dccm */
+ #define BMU_INIT_DEVEL_MODE		BIT(7)	/* init BMU development mode configuration */
+ #define BMU_INIT_PROD_MODE		BIT(8)	/* init BMU production mode configuration */
+ #define CRS_MODE_ENABLED_M		BIT(9) 	/* enable CRS mode */
+ #define SIM_MODE			BIT(10)	/* Simulator mode */
+
+ /* runtime settings for PALAD use only (using spare address in all efuses) */
+ #define EFUSE_PALAD_AVAILABLE_DIES_OFFSET		46
+ #define PALAD_AVAILABLE_DIES_M				GENMASK(7, 0)
+ #define COMPUTE_DIE_0_AVAIL_M				BIT(0)
+ #define COMPUTE_DIE_1_AVAIL_M				BIT(1)
+ #define COMPUTE_DIE_2_AVAIL_M				BIT(2)
+ #define COMPUTE_DIE_3_AVAIL_M				BIT(3)
+ #define IO_DIE_0_AVAIL_M				BIT(4)
+ #define IO_DIE_0_HALF_1_AVAIL_M				BIT(5)
+ #define IO_DIE_1_AVAIL_M				BIT(6)
+ #define IO_DIE_1_HALF_1_AVAIL_M				BIT(7)
+
+ /* EFUSE SYSCFG BIT Definitions start */
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_0			BIT(0)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_0_1			(BIT(0) | BIT(1))
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_0_1_2			(BIT(0) | BIT(1) | BIT(2))
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_0_1_2_3			(BIT(0) | BIT(1) | BIT(2) | BIT(3))
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_4			BIT(4)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_KEY_REVOCATION_BITS		((EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_0_1_2_3) \
+							 | (EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_4))
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_EOM_BIT				BIT(5)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_IB_REC_FW_UPDATE		BIT(6)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_EN_DIE_ID_SECURITY_CHK		BIT(7)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_EN_SECURE_BOOT			BIT(9)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_SIG_VAL_EN				BIT(10)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_TWI				BIT(11)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_JTAG				BIT(12)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_EFUSE_OVERRIDE			BIT(13)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_VOLT_PROT_BITS			(BIT(14) | BIT(15) | BIT(16) | BIT(17))
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_PCIE_GEN_LIMIT			BIT(18)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_SPI_DIRECT_FW_UPDATE		BIT(19)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_BLOCK_ALL_RR			BIT(20)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_SECONDARY_IMG			BIT(21)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_SPI_TPM_BAUDR			BIT(22)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_SKU				(BIT(23) | BIT(24) | BIT(25) | BIT(26))
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_PSOC_SPI_GW_BAUDR			BIT(27)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_PSOC_SPI_GW			BIT(28)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_SPI_TPM_RX_DELAY			BIT(29)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_GPIO01_RECOVERY_CONTROL		BIT(30)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_EROM_I3C_INIT			BIT(31)
+ /* EFUSE SYSCFG BIT Definitions end */
+
+ /* EFUSE SYSCFG 2 BIT Definitions start */
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG2_RECOVERY_CODE_LOCATION		BIT(27)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG2_DISABLE_BOOT_INFO			BIT(28)
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG2_RMA_MODE				BIT(29)
+ /* EFUSE SYSCFG 2 BIT Definitions end */
+
+ /*SPI TPM BAUDR configurations*/
+ enum boot_rom_tpm_spi_freq_config {
+	 FREQ_CFG_12_5MHZ = 0,
+	 FREQ_CFG_6_25MHZ = 1,
+ };
+
+ /* EFUSE SYSCFG2 BIT Definitions start */
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG2_D2D_DISABLE			BIT(31)
+ /* EFUSE SYSCFG2 BIT Definitions end */
+
+ /* EFUSE Shift definitions */
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_SKU_SHIFT				23
+
+ #define EFUSE_SYSCFG_PRE_PRODUCTION_FLAGS		((EFUSE_SYSCFG_EN_SECURE_BOOT \
+							 | EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_EFUSE_OVERRIDE \
+							 | EFUSE_SYSCFG_SIG_VAL_EN \
+							 | EFUSE_SYSCFG_REVOKE_KEY_0 \
+							 | EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_TWI /* TODO: TBD */ \
+							 | EFUSE_SYSCFG_DIS_JTAG)) /* TODO: TBD */
+
+ /* redundnacy value read from EFUSE start */
+ #define EFUSE_IO_VDEC_AND_NIC_REDUN_OFFSET		19
+ #define EFUSE_IO_VDEC_REDUN_MASK			GENMASK(17, 0)
+ #define EFUSE_IO_NIC_REDUN_MASK				GENMASK(31, 19)
+ #define IO_VDEC8_FAULT_MASK_D0_L			GENMASK(8, 0)
+ #define IO_VDEC8_FAULT_MASK_D0_H			GENMASK(17, 9)
+ #define IO_DEC8_FAULT_MASK_DEFAULT			BIT(8)
+ #define IO_NIC12_FAULT_MASK_DEFAULT			BIT(12)
+ #define IO_VDEC8_NUM_PER_HALF_DIE			9
+
+ #define EFUSE_COMPUTE_EDMA_REDN_OFFSET			29
+ #define EFUSE_COMPUTE_EDMA_REDN_MASK			GENMASK(4, 0)
+
+ #define EFUSE_COMPUTE_PDMA_REDN_OFFSET			29
+ #define EFUSE_COMPUTE_PDMA_REDN_MASK			GENMASK(6, 5)
+
+ #define EFUSE_COMPUTE_XECORE_0_REDUN_OFFSET		24
+ #define EFUSE_COMPUTE_XECORE_1_REDUN_OFFSET		26
+
+ #define COMPUTE_EDMA_3_4_FAULT_MASK_DEFAULT		GENMASK(4, 3)
+ #define COMPUTE_PDMA_2_FAULT_MASK_DEFAULT		BIT(2)

b/gpu/jgs/jgs_fw_if.h

@@ -0,0 +1,151 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2025 Intel, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+ #ifndef JGS_FW_IF_H
+ #define JGS_FW_IF_H
+
+ /* rst src bit in PSOC_GLOBAL_CONF_RST_SRC register */
+ enum jgs_rst_src_bit {
+	 HL_COLD_RST,
+	 HL_MANUAL_RST,
+	 HL_PRSTN_RST,
+	 HL_SOFT_RST,
+	 HL_WD_RST,
+	 HL_FW_ALL_RST,
+	 HL_SW_ALL_RST,
+	 HL_FLR_RST,
+	 HL_ECC_DERR_RST,
+	 HL_CXL_RST,
+	 HL_HOT_RST,
+	 HL_VF_FLR_RST,
+	 HL_UNKNOWN_RST = 255
+ };
+
+ /*
+  * CPU ID must be written before the CPU reset is released. Since ARC MNG is the first
+  * to run (by HW), its ID will be the default (0). For the other CPUs a unique ID
+  * is set.
+  */
+ #define IO0_PSOC_ARC_MNG_ID		0
+ #define IO0_PSOC_ARC_RST_ID		1
+ #define IO0_PSOC_ARC_PID_ID		2
+ #define IO0_PSOC_ARC_MISC_ID		3
+ #define IO0_PCIE_ARC_CXL_ID		4
+
+ struct jgs_cold_rst_data {
+	 union {
+		 struct {
+			 uint32_t recovery_flag: 1;
+			 uint32_t validation_flag: 1;
+			 uint32_t valid_manifest_idx: 1;
+			 uint32_t efuse_read_flag: 1;
+			 uint32_t spsram_init_done : 1;
+			 uint32_t arc1_iccm_init_done: 1;
+			 uint32_t tpm_validation_flag: 1;
+			 uint32_t arc1_persist_dccm_init_done: 1;
+			 uint32_t fuse_emulation_init_done: 1;
+			 uint32_t boot_info_set_done: 1;
+			 uint32_t reserved : 22;
+		 };
+		 uint32_t data;
+	 };
+ };
+
+ #define JGS_NUM_HALF_DIES		2
+ #define JGS_NUM_IO_DIES		2
+ #define JGS_NUM_COMPUTE_DIES		4
+ #define JGS_NUM_REDUNDANT_EDMA		2
+
+ #define MAX_DIE_COUNT		(JGS_NUM_IO_DIES + JGS_NUM_COMPUTE_DIES)
+
+ /* In case there is a defect, io die redundant components are
+  * 1 of 9 DEC in each IO Die half
+  * 1 of 13 NICs in each IO Die
+  * PCIE_CLS in IO Die 1
+  * PMMU in IO Die for production (only used in IO Die stand-alone package)
+  * redundancy structure for JGS IO die
+  */
+ struct jgs_redundancy_ctx_io {
+	 uint8_t initialized;		/* track driver initialization */
+	 uint16_t dec_fault_mask[JGS_NUM_HALF_DIES];
+	 uint16_t nic_fault_mask;
+	 uint8_t pmmu_fault_mask;
+	 uint8_t pcie_cls_fault_mask;	/* used only for die 1 */
+
+	 int8_t redundant_dec[JGS_NUM_HALF_DIES];
+	 int8_t redundant_nic;
+	 int8_t redundant_pmmu;
+	 int8_t redundant_die_pcie_cls;	/* used only for die 1 */
+ };
+
+ /* compute die redundant components are
+  * 2 of 5 EDMAs in each Compute Die
+  * 1 of 3 PDMAs in each Compute Die
+  * 1 of every 17 XeCores in Compute Die
+  * 1 CS + 6x HBM PC , AKA Dual plane.
+  * redundancy structure for JGS compute die
+  */
+ struct jgs_redundancy_ctx_compute {
+	 uint8_t initialized;		/* track driver initialization */
+	 uint8_t edma_fault_mask;
+	 uint8_t pdma_fault_mask;
+	 uint32_t xecore_fault_mask;
+
+	 /* TODO  Dual Plane */
+	 int8_t redundant_edma[JGS_NUM_REDUNDANT_EDMA];
+	 int8_t redundant_pdma;
+	 int8_t redundant_xecore;
+ };
+
+ /* redundancy structure for JGS */
+ struct jgs_redundancy_ctx {
+	 struct jgs_redundancy_ctx_io io_die[JGS_NUM_IO_DIES];
+	 struct jgs_redundancy_ctx_compute compute_die[JGS_NUM_COMPUTE_DIES];
+ };
+
+ /*
+  * Note:
+  * IO Half1 Die ID's are Virtual, just used for register offsets.
+  * Actual die ids are same between half0 and half1.
+  */
+ enum jgs_die_id {
+	 JGS_COMPUTE_DIE_0 = 0,
+	 JGS_COMPUTE_DIE_1 = 1,
+	 JGS_COMPUTE_DIE_2 = 2,
+	 JGS_COMPUTE_DIE_3 = 3,
+	 JGS_IO_DIE_0 = 4,
+	 JGS_IO_DIE_0_HALF_1 = 5,	/* Virtual Die ID */
+	 JGS_IO_DIE_1 = 6,
+	 JGS_IO_DIE_1_HALF_1 = 7,	/* Virtual Die ID */
+	 JGS_INVALID_DIE_ID = 8,
+ };
+
+ enum jgs_die_type {
+	 JGS_COMPUTE_DIE,
+	 JGS_IO_DIE_MASTER,
+	 JGS_IO_DIE_SLAVE,
+	 JGS_INVALID_DIE_TYPE,
+ };
+
+ enum jgs_sku_type {
+	 JGS_FULL_PACKAGE,
+	 JGS_PCIE_CARD,
+	 JGS_IO_DIE_SA,
+	 JGS_INVALID_SKU,
+ };
+
+ /* This enum used as index while fake fuse bin creation and to retrieve */
+ enum fake_fuse_bin_index {
+  FAKE_FUSE_IO_D0,
+  FAKE_FUSE_IO_D1,
+  FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D0,
+  FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D1,
+  FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D2,
+  FAKE_FUSE_COMPUTE_D3,
+ };
+
+ #endif /* JGS_FW_IF_H */

+* JGS Dup engine supports two regions with 8 groups each. Region 0
+* On IO die, Region 0 is allocated to mgmt arc, Region 1 is
+* allocated to pid Arc.
+* On Compute die, Region 0 is allocated to mgmt arc, Region 1 is
+* not used
+* DUP_GROUP_MMIO_RANGE = 512, DUP_NUM_GROUPS_PER_REGION =8
+*/
+#define DUP_ENGINE_IO_REGION_0_OFFSET		f9cfc000

+#define DUP_ENGINE_IO_REGION_1_PUSH_BASE	f9cf9000

+/* IO0 HALF 0 SW MBX */

+#define IO0_HALF0_SW_MAILBOX0_0					F9CA7000

+#define IO0_HALF0_SW_MAILBOX0_1					F9CA7004
+/* IO MGMT --> IO PID */
+#define IO0_HALF0_SW_MAILBOX1_0					F9CA8000
+/* IO MGMT --> IO RESET */
+#define IO0_HALF0_SW_MAILBOX1_1					F9CA8004
+/* IO PID --> IO MGMT */
+#define IO0_HALF0_SW_MAILBOX2_0					F9CA9000
+/* IO RESET --> IO MGMT */
+#define IO0_HALF0_SW_MAILBOX2_1					F9CA9004
+
+/* COMPUTE0 SW MBX */
+/* IO MGMT --> CMPT0 MGMT */
+#define COMPUTE0_SW_MAILBOX0_0					F1CA7000
+/* CMPT0 RESET --> CMPT0 MGMT */
+#define COMPUTE0_SW_MAILBOX1_0					F1CA8000
+/* CMPT0 MGMT --> CMPT0 RESET */
+#define COMPUTE0_SW_MAILBOX1_1					F1CA8004
+
+/* COMPUTE1 SW MBX */
+/* IO MGMT --> CMPT1 MGMT */
+#define COMPUTE1_SW_MAILBOX0_0					F3CA7000
+/* CMPT1 RESET --> CMPT0 MGMT */
+#define COMPUTE1_SW_MAILBOX1_0					F3CA8000
+/* CMPT1 MGMT --> CMPT0 RESET */
+#define COMPUTE1_SW_MAILBOX1_1					F3CA8004
+
+/* IO: MGMT <==> PID IPM */
+#define SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE			0x100
+#define SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_IO_PID_RX_MBX		0x78A00000
+#define SPSRAM_IO_PID_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX		(SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_IO_PID_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x78A00100 */
+
+/* IO: MGMT <==> Reset IPM */
+#define SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_IO_RST_RX_MBX		(SPSRAM_IO_PID_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x78A00200 */
+#define SPSRAM_IO_RST_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX		(SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_IO_RST_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x78A00300 */
+
+/* IO: MGMT <==> MISC IPM */
+#define SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_IO_MISC_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_IO_RST_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x78A00400 */
+#define SPSRAM_IO_MISC_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_IO_MISC_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x78A00500 */
+
+/* IO MGMT <==> COMPUTE0 MGMT IPM */
+#define SPSRAM_COMPUTE0_BASE			0x70A00000
+#define SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_CMPT0_MGMT_RX_MBX	SPSRAM_COMPUTE0_BASE
+#define SPSRAM_CMPT0_MGMT_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_CMPT0_MGMT_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x70A00100 */
+/* COMPUTE0: MGMT <==> Reset IPM */
+#define SPSRAM_CMPT0_MGMT_TX_CMPT0_RST_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_CMPT0_MGMT_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX + \
+							SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x70A00200 */
+#define SPSRAM_CMPT0_RST_TX_CMPT0_MGMT_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_CMPT0_MGMT_TX_CMPT0_RST_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x70A00300 */
+
+/* IO MGMT <==> COMPUTE1 MGMT IPM */
+#define SPSRAM_COMPUTE1_BASE			0x72A00000
+#define SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_CMPT1_MGMT_RX_MBX	SPSRAM_COMPUTE1_BASE
+#define SPSRAM_CMPT1_MGMT_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_IO_MGMT_TX_CMPT1_MGMT_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x72A00100 */
+/* COMPUTE1: MGMT <==> Reset IPM */
+#define SPSRAM_CMPT1_MGMT_TX_CMPT1_RST_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_CMPT1_MGMT_TX_IO_MGMT_RX_MBX + \
+							SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x72A00200 */
+#define SPSRAM_CMPT1_RST_TX_CMPT1_MGMT_RX_MBX	(SPSRAM_CMPT1_MGMT_TX_CMPT1_RST_RX_MBX + \
+						SPSRAM_IPM_BUFF_SIZE) /* 0x72A00300 */

+#endif

b/gpu/jgs/soc.h

@@ -0,0 +1,63 @@
+/* SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
+ *
+ * Copyright 2025 Intel, Ltd.
+ * All Rights Reserved.
+ *
+ */
+
+#ifndef _SOC_H_
+#define _SOC_H_
+
+#define MASK_32_BIT			0xFFFFFFFF
+#define MASK_28_BIT			0xFFFFFFF
+
+#define REGION_ICCM_IDX			0
+#define REGION_HBW_0_IDX		1
+#define REGION_HBW_1_IDX		2
+#define REGION_HBW_2_IDX		3
+#define REGION_HBW_3_IDX		4
+#define REGION_HBW_4_IDX		5
+#define REGION_HBW_5_IDX		6
+#define REGION_FW_MEM_IDX		7
+#define REGION_DCCM_IDX			8
+#define REGION_GEN_MEM_IDX		9
+#define REGION_GT_TILE_0_IDX		10
+#define REGION_GT_TILE_1_IDX		11
+#define REGION_GT_TILE_2_IDX		12
+#define REGION_GT_TILE_3_IDX		13
+#define REGION_LBW_DBG_IDX		14
+#define REGION_LBW_CFG_IDX		15
+
+#define REGION_BASE_ADDR(REG_IDX)	(REG_IDX << 28)
+
+#define ARC_REGION_MASK			(REGION_BASE_ADDR(0xF))
+#define LOCAL_ICCM_BASE			(REGION_BASE_ADDR(REGION_ICCM_IDX))
+#define LOCAL_DCCM_BASE			(REGION_BASE_ADDR(REGION_DCCM_IDX))
+#define FW_MEM_REG_BASE			(REGION_BASE_ADDR(REGION_FW_MEM_IDX))
+#define GEN_MEM_REG_BASE		(REGION_BASE_ADDR(REGION_GEN_MEM_IDX))
+#define CFG_SPACE_REG_BASE		(REGION_BASE_ADDR(REGION_LBW_CFG_IDX))
+
+#define FULL_ADDR_MSB			0x300007F
+
+#define CFG_MEM_FULL_BASE		0x300007ff0000000ull
+#define GEN_MEM_FULL_BASE		0x300007f00000000ull
+
+#define GET_FULL_ADDR(REG_OFF)		((REG_OFF & MASK_28_BIT) + CFG_MEM_FULL_BASE)
+
+#define GET_ARC_ADDR(REG_OFF, REGION)   \
+    ((REG_OFF & MASK_28_BIT) + (REGION_BASE_ADDR(REGION)))
+
+#define CHECK_ADDR_IN_RANGE(ADDR, ADDR_O, BASE, BASE_O)	\
+    ((ADDR >= BASE) && ((ADDR + ADDR_O) <= (BASE + BASE_O)))
+
+/* This 2 macro's to convert ARC based 32B address to LBW MEM map address of 32B */
+#define CONV_ARC_REG_TO_GEN_MEM(addr)	(((addr) & MASK_28_BIT) + ((GEN_MEM_FULL_BASE) & MASK_32_BIT))
+#define CONV_ARC_REG_TO_CFG_MEM(addr)	(((addr) & MASK_28_BIT) + ((CFG_MEM_FULL_BASE) & MASK_32_BIT))
+
+#define FLASH_VALID_MASK		(SZ_2M - 1)
+#define SPI_FLASH_OFFSET		0xF000000
+#define SPI_FLASH_SIZE			(16 * SZ_1MB)
+#define SPI_FLASH_BASE			(GEN_MEM_REG_BASE + SPI_FLASH_OFFSET)
+
+#endif /* _SOC_H_ */
+
